 1514	                pass
 1515	        raise error
 1516	
 1517	    def _close_quietly(self, handle, failures, cancellations=None):
 1518	        """Close one handle and name a failure of the close.
 1519	
 1520	        A cancellation in the middle of a close never skips the close
 1521	        that follows it: it is named in the failures and it is handed to
 1522	        the caller, which raises it after every safe step was executed.
 1523	        """
 1524	        try:
 1525	            if not self._api.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle):
 1526	                failures.append('CloseHandle:last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1527	                return False
 1528	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 1529	            failures.append('CloseHandle:CANCELLED')
 1530	            if cancellations is not None:
 1531	                cancellations.append(error)
 1532	            return False
 1533	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 1534	            failures.append('CloseHandle:' + type(error).__name__)
 1535	            return False
 1536	        return True
 1537	
 1538	    def _release_created(self, process, thread, failures, cancellations=None):
 1539	        """Release a process that was created but never adopted.
 1540	
 1541	        The process is stopped through its own original handle, its exit
 1542	        is confirmed and both of its handles are closed.  A failure of
 1543	        one of the steps is named and the other steps still run: a handle
 1544	        is never left to the garbage collector.  A cancellation in the
 1545	        middle of the release never skips the close that follows it: it
 1546	        is named and the release continues.
 1547	
 1548	        The confirmation of the exit and the confirmation of the close of
 1549	        a handle are two separate facts and they are recorded separately:
 1550	        a wait that timed out is not an exit and a close that failed is
 1551	        not a close.  A handle that could not be closed stays in the
 1552	        holding table of the façade: the original reference is kept and
 1553	        the recovery of the façade can reach it.
 1554	
 1555	        The **control handle of the process** is the one exception: it
 1556	        is closed only when the exit was really confirmed.  A process
 1557	        whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive - it may be a
 1558	        suspended process that never ran - and its control handle is the
 1559	        only handle that may stop it.  The handle stays in the holding
 1560	        table of the façade with its owner, so that the recovery of the
 1561	        façade can reach it again.
 1562	
 1563	        The cancellations of the release are collected in the list of
 1564	        the caller: they are raised after every safe step was executed
 1565	        and they never replace the primary error of the creation.
 1566	        """
 1567	        if cancellations is None:
 1568	            cancellations = []
 1569	        try:
 1570	            if not self._api.kernel32.TerminateProcess(process, 1):
 1571	                failures.append('TerminateProcess:last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1572	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 1573	            failures.append('TerminateProcess:CANCELLED')
 1574	            cancellations.append(error)
 1575	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 1576	            failures.append('TerminateProcess:' + type(error).__name__)
 1577	        confirmed = self._confirm_process_exit(process, failures, cancellations)
 1578	        # The thread handle is independent of the exit of the process: it
 1579	        # is closed on its own and it never waits for the process.
 1580	        if thread is not None:
 1581	            if self._close_quietly(thread, failures, cancellations):
 1582	                self._forget(thread)
 1583	            else:
 1584	                self._note(thread, 'close_not_confirmed')
 1585	        if not confirmed:
 1586	            # The process may still be alive.  Its control handle stays
 1587	            # with the façade: it is never closed and never forgotten.
 1588	            failures.append('process_control_handle_retained:exit_not_confirmed')
 1589	            self._note(process, 'exit_not_confirmed:control_handle_retained')
 1590	            if cancellations:
 1591	                failures.append('release_cancelled:'
 1592	                                + type(cancellations[0]).__name__)
 1593	            return failures
 1594	        if self._close_quietly(process, failures, cancellations):
 1595	            self._forget(process)
 1596	        else:
 1597	            self._note(process, 'close_not_confirmed')
 1598	        if cancellations:
 1599	            failures.append('release_cancelled:' + type(cancellations[0]).__name__)
 1600	        return failures
 1601	
 1602	    def _process_creation_time(self, process):
 1603	        """The birth time of one process, as the 100 nanosecond tick count.
 1604	
 1605	        The query is the identity of the process: a process whose birth
 1606	        time cannot be read has no proven identity and is not used.
 1607	        """
 1608	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
 1609	        creation = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
 1610	        exit_time = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
 1611	        kernel = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
 1612	        user = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
 1613	        if not self._api.kernel32.GetProcessTimes(
 1614	                process, ctypes.byref(creation), ctypes.byref(exit_time),
 1615	                ctypes.byref(kernel), ctypes.byref(user)):
 1616	            error = self._last_error()
 1617	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PROCESS_BIRTH_QUERY_FAILED',
 1618	                              'last_error=%d' % error)
 1619	        return (int(creation.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | int(creation.dwLowDateTime)
 1620	
 1621	    def assign_process_to_job(self, job, process):
 1622	        if not self._api.kernel32.AssignProcessToJobObject(job, process):
 1623	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_ASSIGN_FAILED',
 1624	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1625	        return True
 1626	
 1627	    def resume_thread(self, thread):
 1628	        result = self._api.kernel32.ResumeThread(thread)
 1629	        if result == 0xFFFFFFFF:
 1630	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_RESUME_FAILED',
 1631	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1632	        return int(result)
 1633	
 1634	    def terminate_process(self, process, exit_code):
 1635	        if not self._api.kernel32.TerminateProcess(process, exit_code):
 1636	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_TERMINATE_FAILED',
 1637	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1638	        return True
 1639	
 1640	    def wait_process(self, process, timeout_ms):
 1641	        result = self._api.kernel32.WaitForSingleObject(process, timeout_ms)
 1642	        if result == self.WAIT_OBJECT_0:
 1643	            return True
 1644	        if result == self.WAIT_TIMEOUT:
 1645	            return False
 1646	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_WAIT_FAILED', 'wait=%d' % int(result))
 1647	
 1648	    def exit_code(self, process):
 1649	        code = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
 1650	        if not self._api.kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess(
 1651	                process, self._api.types['ctypes'].byref(code)):
 1652	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_EXIT_CODE_FAILED',
 1653	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1654	        return int(code.value)
 1655	
 1656	    def close_handle(self, handle):
 1657	        if not self._api.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle):
 1658	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED',
 1659	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1660	        # The close was confirmed: the handle leaves the holding table
 1661	        # of the façade.  A handle that could not be closed stays in it
 1662	        # with its owner and it is reachable by the recovery.
 1663	        self._forget(handle)
 1664	        return True
 1665	
 1666	    # -- the pipes ---------------------------------------------------------
 1667	    def create_pipe(self, name):
 1668	        """Create one pipe.  Only the write end is inheritable.
 1669	
 1670	        The security attributes of the pipe say that the ends are
 1671	        inheritable and the inherit flag of the *read* end is cleared
 1672	        right afterwards.  A pipe that was created with ``NULL``
 1673	        security attributes can never be inherited at all.
 1674	
 1675	        Both ends enter the holding table of this façade in the moment
 1676	        of their creation.  A pipe whose inherit flag could not be
 1677	        cleared therefore keeps its original ends with their owner: the
 1678	        core never received the pair and the round never loses the
 1679	        ownership of a handle that it created.
 1680	
 1681	        The failure of the inherit flag is cleaned up **independently**
 1682	        of the two ends.  A cancellation of the inherit flag and a
 1683	        cancellation of the close of one end never skip the close of the
 1684	        other end: the safe close of both ends always runs and the
 1685	        cancellation is raised afterwards.  The end that could not be
 1686	        closed keeps its original reference with the façade and the end
 1687	        that was closed with a confirmed close leaves the table.
 1688	        """
 1689	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
 1690	        read = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
 1691	        write = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
 1692	        attributes = self._security_attributes(True)
 1693	        if not self._api.kernel32.CreatePipe(ctypes.byref(read), ctypes.byref(write),
 1694	                                             ctypes.byref(attributes), 0):
 1695	            error = self._last_error()
 1696	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_FAILED', 'last_error=%d' % error)
 1697	        # The moment of the creation of the two ends.
 1698	        self._retain(read, 'pipe_read')
 1699	        self._retain(write, 'pipe_write')
 1700	        primary = None
 1701	        inherited = False
 1702	        try:
 1703	            inherited = bool(self._api.kernel32.SetHandleInformation(
 1704	                read, self.HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0))
 1705	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 1706	            # The cancellation of the inherit flag is the primary error
 1707	            # of the pipe and it never skips the safe close of the ends.
 1708	            primary = error
 1709	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 1710	            primary = BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_INHERIT_FAILED',
 1711	                                  'last_error=%d:%s' % (self._last_error(),
 1712	                                                        type(error).__name__))
 1713	        if inherited:
 1714	            return read, write
 1715	        error = self._last_error()
 1716	        failures = []
 1717	        cancellations = []
 1718	        if self._close_quietly(read, failures, cancellations):
 1719	            self._forget(read)
 1720	        if self._close_quietly(write, failures, cancellations):
 1721	            self._forget(write)
 1722	        if primary is None:
 1723	            primary = BrokerError(
 1724	                'BROKER_PIPE_INHERIT_FAILED',
 1725	                'last_error=%d%s' % (error,
 1726	                                     ('|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
 1727	                                     if failures else ''))
 1728	        else:
 1729	            try:
 1730	                primary.detail = ((getattr(primary, 'detail', None) or '')
 1731	                                  + '|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
 1732	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 1733	                pass
 1734	        if cancellations:
 1735	            # The cancellation of a close is the error that leaves the
 1736	            # method and the primary error stays reachable next to it:
 1737	            # a cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence and it
 1738	            # never hides the error that caused the pipe.
 1739	            cancel = cancellations[0]
 1740	            try:
 1741	                cancel.lvw_primary = primary
 1742	                primary.lvw_cancelled = cancel
 1743	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 1744	                pass
 1745	            raise cancel
 1746	        raise primary
 1747	
 1748	    def read_stream(self, handle, size):
 1749	        """Read one block of a child stream.
 1750	
 1751	        The end of a stream is an end and not an error, but only in the
 1752	        branches that Windows documents as the end of a pipe: a broken
 1753	        pipe, a pipe that is not connected any more, the end of the file
 1754	        and an operation that was aborted.  Every other failure of the
 1755	        read is a failure of the round and it is named.
 1756	        """
 1757	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
 1758	        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(size)
 1759	        read = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
 1760	        if not self._api.kernel32.ReadFile(handle, buffer, size,
 1761	                                           ctypes.byref(read), None):
 1762	            error = self._last_error()
 1763	            if error in (self.ERROR_BROKEN_PIPE, self.ERROR_PIPE_NOT_CONNECTED,
 1764	                         self.ERROR_HANDLE_EOF, self.ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED):
 1765	                return b''
 1766	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_READ_FAILED', 'last_error=%d' % error)
 1767	        return buffer.raw[:read.value]
 1768	
 1769	    def write_stream(self, handle, data):
 1770	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
 1771	        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(bytes(data), len(data))
 1772	        written = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
 1773	        if not self._api.kernel32.WriteFile(handle, buffer, len(data),
 1774	                                            ctypes.byref(written), None):
 1775	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
 1776	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1777	        return int(written.value)
 1778	
 1779	    # -- the cancellation of a synchronous operation -----------------------
 1780	    def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
 1781	        """The original handle of the calling thread.
 1782	
 1783	        The handle is the handle of the thread itself and never a self
 1784	        reported identifier: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` cancels the
 1785	        operation of one thread and it needs the real handle of that
 1786	        thread.
 1787	        """
 1788	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
 1789	        thread = self._api.kernel32.GetCurrentThread()
 1790	        process = self._api.kernel32.GetCurrentProcess()
 1791	        duplicate = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
 1792	        if not self._api.kernel32.DuplicateHandle(process, thread, process,
 1793	                                                  ctypes.byref(duplicate), 0,
 1794	                                                  False, self.DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS):
 1795	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_THREAD_HANDLE_FAILED',
 1796	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
 1797	        return duplicate
 1798	
 1799	    def cancel_synchronous_io(self, thread_handle):
 1800	        """Ask for the cancellation of the synchronous operation of a thread.
 1801	
 1802	        The call is a **request** and not a completion.  It may report
 1803	        ``ERROR_NOT_FOUND`` because the operation had not started yet or
 1804	        because it had just finished: the caller has to read the real
 1805	        completion of the thread and to join it.  The return value of
 1806	        this method names the state of the request.
 1807	        """
 1808	        if not self._api.kernel32.CancelSynchronousIo(thread_handle):
 1809	            error = self._last_error()
 1810	            if error == self.ERROR_NOT_FOUND:
 1811	                return 'NOT_FOUND'
 1812	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_FAILED',
 1813	                              'last_error=%d' % error)
 1814	        return 'REQUESTED'
 1815	
 1816	
 1817	# -- the fixed command of a role ------------------------------------------
 1818	
 1819	def _base_environment():
 1820	    environment = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items()
 1821	                   if key.upper() not in REMOVED_ENVIRONMENT}
 1822	    environment['PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE'] = '1'
 1823	    environment['PYTHONNOUSERSITE'] = '1'
 1824	    environment['NO_PROXY'] = '127.0.0.1,localhost'
 1825	    return environment
 1826	
 1827	
 1828	def _comfy_environment(root):
 1829	    environment = _base_environment()
 1830	    temporary = str(Path(root) / 'temp' / 'service-os')
 1831	    environment.update({
 1832	        'HF_HUB_OFFLINE': '1',
 1833	        'TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE': '1',
 1834	        'HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE': '1',
 1835	        'TEMP': temporary,
 1836	        'TMP': temporary,
 1837	        'TORCH_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'torch'),
 1838	        'HF_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'huggingface'),
 1839	        'XDG_CACHE_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'cache'),
 1840	    })
 1841	    return environment
 1842	
 1843	
 1844	def _ollama_environment():
 1845	    environment = _base_environment()
 1846	    environment['OLLAMA_HOST'] = '127.0.0.1:11434'
 1847	    environment['OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD'] = '1'
 1848	    return environment
 1849	
 1850	
 1851	def role_command(role, run_root, owned_pids):
 1852	    """Assemble the fixed command of one role.
 1853	
 1854	    The command form of a role is frozen in this module and it is
 1855	    assembled from the tools that the plan closed.  The plan never carries
 1856	    an argument vector, a shell, a script or an environment: it carries
 1857	    the tools and the closed data of the role, and this function is the
 1858	    only place where a command line of this package exists.
 1859	
 1860	    The forms are the ones of the already authorised launch of this pack:
 1861	    the ComfyUI launch of ``reports/v0-s1/wp-s1-03-phase-a/live_probe.py``
 1862	    and of ``tools/v0_s2_driver.py``, the Ollama launch of the same
 1863	    wrapper, and the sampler launch of ``tools/v0_s2_runtime.py``.
 1864	
 1865	    The private process ids of the sampler are passed as *separate*
 1866	    arguments: the parser of the sampler is ``type=int, nargs='+'`` and
 1867	    one argument that carries a space would not be an integer.
 1868	    """
 1869	    name = role['role']
 1870	    tools = role['tools']
 1871	    data = role['data']
 1872	    root = str(run_root)
 1873	    if name == 'COMFY':
 1874	        python = tools[0]['path']
 1875	        main = tools[1]['path']
 1876	        arguments = ['-s', '-B', main,
 1877	                     '--listen', '127.0.0.1', '--port', '8191',
 1878	                     '--base-directory', root,
 1879	                     '--user-directory', str(Path(root) / data['user_directory']),
 1880	                     '--disable-auto-launch', '--disable-api-nodes',
 1881	                     '--disable-all-custom-nodes',
 1882	                     '--whitelist-custom-nodes', 'lvw_v0_bridge']
 1883	        return {'executable': python, 'arguments': arguments,
 1884	                'working_directory': root, 'environment': _comfy_environment(root)}
 1885	    if name == 'OLLAMA':
 1886	        executable = tools[0]['path']
 1887	        return {'executable': executable, 'arguments': ['serve'],
 1888	                'working_directory': root, 'environment': _ollama_environment()}
 1889	    if name == 'SAMPLER':
 1890	        python = tools[0]['path']
 1891	        script = tools[1]['path']
 1892	        directory = str(Path(root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler')
 1893	        arguments = ['-I', '-B', script, '--execute-sampler',
 1894	                     '--run-root', directory,
 1895	                     '--nvidia-smi', data['nvidia_exe'],
 1896	                     '--binary-sha256', data['nvidia_sha256'],
 1897	                     '--private-pids'] + [str(pid) for pid in owned_pids] + \
 1898	                    ['--max-seconds', str(data['max_seconds'])]
 1899	        return {'executable': python, 'arguments': arguments,
 1900	                'working_directory': directory, 'environment': _base_environment()}
 1901	    raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_UNKNOWN', name)
 1902	
 1903	
 1904	# -- the bounded reader of a child stream ----------------------------------
 1905	
 1906	class _BoundedStreamReader(threading.Thread):
 1907	    """One bounded background reader of one stream of a child.
 1908	
 1909	    The thread only reads.  It never writes, it never calls the broker and
 1910	    it never waits for the broker.  The broker takes the published counts
 1911	    out of it at its own checkpoints, so the deadline of the round is never
 1912	    held up by a silent child.
 1913	
 1914	    A read that failed for a reason that Windows does not document as the
 1915	    end of a pipe is a named error of the round: the reader keeps it and
 1916	    the round is not a release.
 1917	    """
 1918	
 1919	    def __init__(self, win32, handle, *, limit, name):
 1920	        super().__init__(daemon=True, name='lvw-native-custody-stream-' + name)
 1921	        self.win32 = win32
 1922	        self.handle = handle
 1923	        self.limit = limit
 1924	        self.observed = 0
 1925	        self.kept = 0
 1926	        self.overflow = False
 1927	        self.error = None
 1928	        self.finished = threading.Event()
 1929	
 1930	    def run(self):
 1931	        try:
 1932	            while True:
 1933	                block = self.win32.read_stream(self.handle, 65536)
 1934	                if not block:
 1935	                    break
 1936	                self.observed += len(block)
 1937	                if self.observed > self.limit:
 1938	                    self.overflow = True
 1939	                    break
 1940	                self.kept += len(block)
 1941	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 1942	            self.error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
 1943	        finally:
 1944	            self.finished.set()
 1945	
 1946	
 1947	# -- the pending resources of one round ------------------------------------
 1948	
 1949	class _PendingResources:
 1950	    """The pending resource table of one round.
 1951	
 1952	    Every handle that this round creates enters this table in the moment
 1953	    of its successful creation.  A handle **stays** in the table until it
 1954	    is closed with a confirmed close: the handover of a handle to its
 1955	    owner changes the owner and nothing else.  The handle is a resource
 1956	    that this round owns and it is named until it is really closed.
 1957	
 1958	    A handle that could not be closed stays in the table with its owner:
 1959	    it is a resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
 1960	    evidence of the round.
 1961	
 1962	    A handle of another owner is never in this table and it is never
 1963	    touched.
 1964	
 1965	    The table carries the **owner object** of a handle and not only its
 1966	    name: a handle that is owned by a live reader is protected from the
 1967	    pending pass by the fact that the reader is alive and not by a
 1968	    string that somebody may write.  The owner object is the reader
 1969	    itself and the table asks it.
 1970	    """
 1971	
 1972	    def __init__(self, win32):
 1973	        self._win32 = win32
 1974	        self._items = []
 1975	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
 1976	
 1977	    def add(self, handle, kind, *, role=None, owner=None, owner_ref=None,
 1978	            closeable=None):
 1979	        """Enter one handle into the table of the round.
 1980	
 1981	        The ``closeable`` predicate is the **close eligibility** of the
 1982	        handle.  It is asked by every close of the table and it is the
 1983	        authority of the question "may this handle be closed now?": a
 1984	        control handle of a process whose exit was not confirmed is
 1985	        never closed, and the predicate is the **owner** of that fact
 1986	        and not a name of it.
 1987	
 1988	        The ``owner_ref`` is the object of the owner of the handle: the
 1989	        pending pass asks it before it closes the handle.
 1990	        """
 1991	        item = {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind, 'role': role,
 1992	                'owner': owner or 'PENDING', 'owner_ref': owner_ref,
 1993	                'closeable': closeable, 'closed': False}
 1994	        with self._lock:
 1995	            self._items.append(item)
 1996	        return item
 1997	
 1998	    def take(self, handle):
 1999	        """Remove one handle from the table: its owner holds it now."""
 2000	        with self._lock:
 2001	            for index, item in enumerate(self._items):
 2002	                if item['handle'] == handle:
 2003	                    del self._items[index]
 2004	                    return item
 2005	        return None
 2006	
 2007	    def find(self, handle):
 2008	        """The entry of one handle, without removing it from the table.
 2009	
 2010	        The entry carries the **owner** of the handle and its close
 2011	        eligibility.  A close that is confirmed needs the owner of the
 2012	        handle in order to notify it: a notification that names a copy
 2013	        of the entry would notify nobody.
 2014	        """
 2015	        with self._lock:
 2016	            for item in self._items:
 2017	                if item['handle'] == handle:
 2018	                    return item
 2019	        return None
 2020	
 2021	    def transfer(self, handle, owner, *, owner_ref=None):
 2022	        """Hand one pending handle over to its owner.
 2023	
 2024	        The owner of the handle is only changed after the handover
 2025	        succeeded.  The handle stays in the table until it is closed: it
 2026	        is a resource that this round owns and it is named until then.
 2027	        A handle that could not be handed over keeps the owner
 2028	        ``PENDING`` and it is closed by the round.
 2029	
 2030	        The ``owner_ref`` is the **object** of the new owner.  It is
 2031	        what the pending pass asks before it closes a handle: a read end
 2032	        that a live reader still reads from is never closed under it.
 2033	        """
 2034	        with self._lock:
 2035	            for item in self._items:
 2036	                if item['handle'] == handle:
 2037	                    item['owner'] = owner
 2038	                    if owner_ref is not None:
 2039	                        item['owner_ref'] = owner_ref
 2040	                    return item
 2041	        return None
 2042	
 2043	    def pending(self):
 2044	        """The handles that this round still owns and has not closed."""
 2045	        with self._lock:
 2046	            return [dict(item) for item in self._items]
 2047	
 2048	    def close_all(self, failures, on_closed=None, protect=None):
 2049	        """Close every handle that is still pending and name the failures.
 2050	
 2051	        A handle that could not be closed stays in the table: it is a
 2052	        resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
 2053	        evidence of the round.  It is never left to the garbage
 2054	        collector and it is never closed twice.
 2055	
 2056	        Every handle is closed on its own: a cancellation in the middle
 2057	        of the group never skips the handles that follow it.  The
 2058	        cancellation is raised after every handle was coordinated and it
 2059	        is never turned into an ordinary evidence.
 2060	
 2061	        A handle that was closed with a **confirmed** close is reported
 2062	        to the owner of the table through ``on_closed``: the core has to
 2063	        learn that the handle is really closed, so that it never names a
 2064	        closed handle any more.  The report happens before the
 2065	        cancellation is raised: the bookkeeping of the core is never
 2066	        left behind the error that left the cleanup.
 2067	
 2068	        The ``protect`` predicate is the **real** guard of the pass: a
 2069	        handle whose owner is still using it is never closed under it,
 2070	        whatever the branch that reached the pass did before.  The
 2071	        guard is asked about the owner and not about a name of an owner.
 2072	
 2073	        The **close eligibility** of a handle is asked here as well: a
 2074	        handle that may not be closed yet - the control handle of a
 2075	        process whose exit was not confirmed - is never closed by the
 2076	        pass.  The eligibility comes from the **same** owner that the
 2077	        release of the records asks: the pass may not decide the
 2078	        question on its own and it may not close behind the back of the
 2079	        owner of the handle.
 2080	        """
 2081	        remaining = []
 2082	        cancellations = []
 2083	        with self._lock:
 2084	            items = list(self._items)
 2085	        for item in items:
 2086	            if protect is not None and protect(item):
 2087	                # The handle is in use by a live owner: it is never
 2088	                # closed under it and it keeps its owner.
 2089	                remaining.append(item)
 2090	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2091	                                 'role': item['role'],
 2092	                                 'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_HANDLE_IN_USE'})
 2093	                continue
 2094	            if not self._is_closeable(item):
 2095	                # The handle may not be closed yet: it keeps its owner
 2096	                # and the round keeps the fact.
 2097	                remaining.append(item)
 2098	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2099	                                 'role': item['role'],
 2100	                                 'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_CLOSE_NOT_ELIGIBLE'})
 2101	                continue
 2102	            try:
 2103	                self._win32.close_handle(item['handle'])
 2104	            except BrokerError as error:
 2105	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2106	                                 'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
 2107	                remaining.append(item)
 2108	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 2109	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2110	                                 'role': item['role'],
 2111	                                 'error_type': type(error).__name__,
 2112	                                 'cancellation': True})
 2113	                remaining.append(item)
 2114	                cancellations.append(error)
 2115	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2116	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2117	                                 'role': item['role'],
 2118	                                 'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 2119	                remaining.append(item)
 2120	            else:
 2121	                item['closed'] = True
 2122	                if on_closed is not None:
 2123	                    on_closed(item)
 2124	        with self._lock:
 2125	            self._items = remaining
 2126	        if cancellations:
 2127	            raise cancellations[0]
 2128	        return remaining
 2129	
 2130	    @staticmethod
 2131	    def _is_closeable(item):
 2132	        """Whether one pending handle may be closed now.
 2133	
 2134	        The predicate of the entry is the authority of the question.  A
 2135	        predicate that cannot answer protects the handle: an unknown
 2136	        fact is never a permission to close.
 2137	        """
 2138	        predicate = item.get('closeable')
 2139	        if predicate is None:
 2140	            return True
 2141	        try:
 2142	            return bool(predicate())
 2143	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2144	            return False
 2145	
 2146	
 2147	# -- the core of the broker ------------------------------------------------
 2148	
 2149	class BrokerCore:
 2150	    """The job and the handle custody of one native custody round.
 2151	
 2152	    The core owns exactly one job object and the original handles of the
 2153	    processes of the round.  Every process of the round is created
 2154	    suspended, it is assigned to the job of the round and only then it is
 2155	    resumed.
 2156	
 2157	    A failure never falls back to a weaker behaviour.  There is no retry
 2158	    without the job, there is no adoption of a process that was not
 2159	    created here and there is no stop of a process that this core does not
 2160	    hold a handle of.
 2161	    """
 2162	
 2163	    def __init__(self, *, win32, plan, run_root, clock=None, wall=None,
 2164	                 context=None):
 2165	        self.win32 = win32
 2166	        self.plan = plan
 2167	        self.run_root = Path(run_root)
 2168	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
 2169	        self._wall = wall or time.time
 2170	        # The ONE context of the round.  The core, the loop, the
 2171	        # transport of the channel and the endpoints of the channel hold
 2172	        # this very object: a layer that only copied the number of a
 2173	        # deadline would be a layer with a deadline of its own.
 2174	        self.context = context if context is not None else _RoundContext(
 2175	            clock=self._clock, wall=self._wall)
 2176	        # The façade of the primitives reads the absolute deadline and
 2177	        # the published cleanup deadline of the round from the **same**
 2178	        # context: a recovery of the façade may not open a budget of
 2179	        # its own.
 2180	        binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_context', None)
 2181	        if callable(binder):
 2182	            try:
 2183	                binder(self.context)
 2184	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2185	                pass
 2186	        self.state = 'NEW'
 2187	        self.job = None
 2188	        self.job_kill_on_close = False
 2189	        self.job_closed = False
 2190	        self.job_terminated = False
 2191	        self.job_active = None
 2192	        self.job_active_known = False
 2193	        self.job_process_ids = []
 2194	        self.records = []
 2195	        self.failures = []
 2196	        self.readers = []
 2197	        self.reader_states = []
 2198	        self.new_text_bytes = 0
 2199	        self.stop_reason = None
 2200	        self._stream_failure_reported = False
 2201	        self._text_failure_reported = False
 2202	        self._pending = _PendingResources(win32)
 2203	        # The resources that this core could not release.  A resource
 2204	        # that could not be closed keeps its original reference and its
 2205	        # owner here: it is named in the evidence of the round and it is
 2206	        # never replaced by a copy of an error message.
 2207	        self._retained_resources = []
 2208	        # The cancellations that were observed during the coordination of
 2209	        # the readers.  They are raised after every reader was
 2210	        # coordinated and they never replace an ordinary failure.
 2211	        self._readers_cancelled = []
 2212	        # The cancellations that were observed during the wait of the
 2213	        # original processes.  They are raised after every process was
 2214	        # coordinated.
 2215	        self._processes_cancelled = []
 2216	        # The absolute deadline of the round.  It is bound by the loop
 2217	        # before the job of the round exists and it is the upper bound of
 2218	        # the one cleanup deadline of the round.
 2219	        self._absolute_deadline = None
 2220	        # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated at the
 2221	        # first stop or at the first failure of the cleanup and it is the
 2222	        # same value in every step of the cleanup.
 2223	        self._cleanup_deadline_value = None
 2224	        self._released = False
 2225	        self._abort_reported = False
 2226	        self.broker_identity = {
 2227	            'pid': os.getpid(),
 2228	            'executable': sys.executable,
 2229	            'reported_by_the_broker': True,
 2230	            'identity_source': 'CHILD_CLAIM',
 2231	        }
 2232	
 2233	    # -- the deadlines of the round ---------------------------------------
 2234	    def bind_clock(self, clock):
 2235	        """Bind the clock of the round to this core.
 2236	
 2237	        The core, the loop and the context of the round read the same
 2238	        clock: the absolute deadline of the round and the ONE cleanup
 2239	        deadline are in one domain and a deadline of one domain is never
 2240	        compared with a clock of another one.
 2241	        """
 2242	        if clock is not None:
 2243	            self._clock = clock
 2244	            self.context.clock = clock
 2245	        return self._clock
 2246	
 2247	    def bind_absolute_deadline(self, deadline):
 2248	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to this core.
 2249	
 2250	        The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
 2251	        bound.  It is the upper bound of the one cleanup deadline of the
 2252	        round: the cleanup never outlives the round itself.
 2253	        """
 2254	        value = self.context.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
 2255	        self._absolute_deadline = value
 2256	        # The façade of the primitives is bound to the same deadline: its
 2257	        # waits are bounded by the deadline of the round and they never
 2258	        # start a wait with a fresh timeout of their own.
 2259	        binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_deadline', None)
 2260	        if callable(binder):
 2261	            try:
 2262	                binder(value, self._clock)
 2263	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2264	                pass
 2265	        return value
 2266	
 2267	    def cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
 2268	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of this round.
 2269	
 2270	        The deadline is generated once, at the first stop or at the first
 2271	        failure of the cleanup, and it is published **atomically** by the
 2272	        context of the round.  Two callers that entered the cleanup at
 2273	        the same moment therefore see the very same value and the
 2274	        deadline is never generated twice.
 2275	
 2276	        It is the earlier of the absolute deadline of the round and the
 2277	        fixed cleanup budget, measured from the moment of the first
 2278	        cleanup.  Every later call returns the very same value: no layer
 2279	        of the cleanup adds a budget of its own and no layer starts a
 2280	        new wait with a fresh timeout.
 2281	        """
 2282	        value = self.context.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
 2283	        self._cleanup_deadline_value = value
 2284	        return value
 2285	
 2286	    def remaining_cleanup_seconds(self, deadline=None):
 2287	        """The seconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
 2288	
 2289	        The value is recomputed on every call: a wait that already
 2290	        consumed part of the budget never gets the whole budget again.
 2291	        """
 2292	        return self.context.remaining_cleanup(deadline)
 2293	
 2294	    def check_startup_budget(self, stage):
 2295	        """Check the shared budget of the startup **before** a step.
 2296	
 2297	        A step of the startup of the round - the creation of a process,
 2298	        the assignment of a process to the job, the resume of its
 2299	        thread, the start of a reader and the creation of the next role
 2300	        - checks the shared cancel and the shared deadline of the round
 2301	        before it happens and after it happened.
 2302	
 2303	        A step that runs after the deadline arrived may only clean up:
 2304	        it never resumes a thread and it never creates the next role.
 2305	        """
 2306	        context = self.context
 2307	        if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
 2308	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
 2309	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
 2310	        if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
 2311	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
 2312	        return True
 2313	
 2314	    def _retain_resource(self, kind, handle, code=None, owner='CORE_RETAINED'):
 2315	        """Keep the original reference of a resource that was not released."""
 2316	        for item in self._retained_resources:
 2317	            if item['kind'] == kind and item['handle'] is handle:
 2318	                return item
 2319	        item = {'kind': kind, 'handle': handle, 'owner': owner, 'code': code}
 2320	        self._retained_resources.append(item)
 2321	        return item
 2322	
 2323	    def _release_resource(self, kind, handle):
 2324	        """Forget a resource that was released with a confirmed close.
 2325	
 2326	        The **original reference** of a resource is its identity: a
 2327	        handle that was really closed is forgotten whatever name it was
 2328	        retained under.  A resource that was not closed keeps its owner
 2329	        and it stays named: the retained list is never cleared to make a
 2330	        check green.
 2331	        """
 2332	        self._retained_resources = [item for item in self._retained_resources
 2333	                                   if item['handle'] is not handle]
 2334	
 2335	    def _adopt_from_api(self, entries, role):
 2336	        """Take the named handles over from the façade of the primitives.
 2337	
 2338	        The receiver registers the handles and the façade forgets them
 2339	        in **one** atomic step.  A handle that both of them names would
 2340	        have two independent owners and the recovery of the façade could
 2341	        close a handle that a live reader of the round is reading from.
 2342	        A handle that left the façade before its receiver registered it
 2343	        would have no owner at all.
 2344	
 2345	        The façade that does not offer the atomic handover is asked to
 2346	        forget the handles after the registration: the receiver is
 2347	        always registered first, so that a failure in between leaves the
 2348	        handle with **two** owners and never with none.
 2349	        """
 2350	        items = list(entries)
 2351	
 2352	        def register(handles):
 2353	            for handle, kind in handles:
 2354	                self._pending.add(handle, kind, role=role)
 2355	
 2356	        handover = getattr(self.win32, 'handover', None)
 2357	        if callable(handover):
 2358	            handover(items, owner='CORE', register=register)
 2359	            return
 2360	        register(items)
 2361	        forget = getattr(self.win32, 'forget_handle', None)
 2362	        if callable(forget):
 2363	            for handle, _kind in items:
 2364	                forget(handle)
 2365	
 2366	    def _api_retained(self):
 2367	        """The original handles that the Windows façade still holds.
 2368	
 2369	        The façade is the owner of a handle that it could not hand over
 2370	        and it is the owner of the recovery of that handle.  The core
 2371	        keeps the façade, so the core can always reach these handles.
 2372	
 2373	        The names of the handles are the printable ones: the evidence of
 2374	        a round is a document and it never carries a raw handle value.
 2375	        """
 2376	        getter = getattr(self.win32, 'retained_handles', None)
 2377	        if not callable(getter):
 2378	            return []
 2379	        try:
 2380	            entries = list(getter())
 2381	        except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2382	            return []
 2383	        return [{'kind': entry.get('kind'), 'pid': entry.get('pid'),
 2384	                 'stage': entry.get('stage'), 'owner': entry.get('owner'),
 2385	                 'failures': list(entry.get('failures', ())),
 2386	                 'handle_repr': repr(entry.get('handle'))}
 2387	                for entry in entries]
 2388	
 2389	    # -- the arming of the round ------------------------------------------
 2390	    def arm(self):
 2391	        """Create the job object of the round.
 2392	
 2393	        The job is created before any process of the round exists, so that
 2394	        every process of the round can be assigned to it before it runs.
 2395	
 2396	        The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
 2397	        creation of the job and **after** it: a job that was created
 2398	        after the deadline of the round arrived is only cleaned up and it
 2399	        is never armed.
 2400	        """
 2401	        if self.state != 'NEW':
 2402	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
 2403	        self.state = 'VALIDATED'
 2404	        self.check_startup_budget('ARM:JOB')
 2405	        try:
 2406	            self.job = self.win32.create_job()
 2407	        except BrokerError as error:
 2408	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_job', 'code': error.code})
 2409	            self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
 2410	            raise
 2411	        self._pending.add(self.job, 'job')
 2412	        self._pending.transfer(self.job, 'CORE')
 2413	        try:
 2414	            self.win32.set_job_kill_on_close(self.job)
 2415	        except BrokerError as error:
 2416	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'set_job_limits', 'code': error.code})
 2417	            closed = self._close_handle('job', self.job)
 2418	            if closed:
 2419	                # The job handle was closed with a confirmed close: it may
 2420	                # be forgotten.
 2421	                self.job = None
 2422	            else:
 2423	                # The job handle was not closed.  The original reference
 2424	                # stays with its owner and it is named: a handle that may
 2425	                # not be closed is never forgotten and it is never
 2426	                # replaced by a copy of an error message.
 2427	                self._retain_resource('job', self.job, error.code)
 2428	            self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
 2429	            raise
 2430	        self.job_kill_on_close = True
 2431	        self.state = 'ARMED'
 2432	        # The budget is checked after the creation of the job: a job that
 2433	        # was created after the deadline arrived is only cleaned up.
 2434	        self.check_startup_budget('ARM:DONE')
 2435	        return self.evidence()
 2436	
 2437	    # -- the launch of the roles ------------------------------------------
 2438	    def launch(self):
 2439	        """Create the roles of the round, one after the other.
 2440	
 2441	        The launch has an independent ``finally``: a failure of a role,
 2442	        a refusal of the broker and a cancellation all release the
 2443	        resources of the round before the failure leaves this method.
 2444	        The cancellation itself is not swallowed: it continues to
 2445	        propagate after the cleanup.
 2446	        """
 2447	        if self.state != 'ARMED':
 2448	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
 2449	        try:
 2450	            for role in self.plan.roles:
 2451	                # The shared budget of the startup is checked before
 2452	                # every role: a role that is created after the deadline
 2453	                # of the round arrived is a role that nobody may stop.
 2454	                self.check_startup_budget('ROLE:' + role['role'])
 2455	                self._launch_role(role)
 2456	        except BaseException:
 2457	            # Any failure of the launch, a cancellation included, ends
 2458	            # the round closed: the job, the pipes and the original
 2459	            # handles of this round are released here.
 2460	            self._abort_round('LAUNCH_ABORTED')
 2461	            raise
 2462	        self.state = 'RUNNING'
 2463	        return self.evidence()
 2464	
 2465	    def _launch_role(self, role):
 2466	        name = role['role']
 2467	        owned = [record['pid'] for record in self.records if record['pid']]
 2468	        command = role_command(role, self.run_root, owned)
 2469	        record = {'role': name,
 2470	                  'executable': command['executable'],
 2471	                  'executable_sha256': role['tools'][0]['sha256'],
 2472	                  'arguments': list(command['arguments']),
 2473	                  'working_directory': command['working_directory'],
 2474	                  'log': role['log'],
 2475	                  'created': False, 'assigned': False, 'resumed': False,
 2476	                  'pid': None, 'creation_time': None,
 2477	                  'terminated': False, 'exited': False, 'exit_code': None,
 2478	                  'process_handle_closed': False, 'thread_handle_closed': False,
 2479	                  'stdout_bytes': 0, 'stderr_bytes': 0,
 2480	                  'stdout_overflow': False, 'stderr_overflow': False,
 2481	                  'failure': None,
 2482	                  'pending_handles': []}
 2483	        self.records.append(record)
 2484	        try:
 2485	            self._prepare_directories(role)
 2486	        except OSError as error:
 2487	            record['failure'] = 'BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED'
 2488	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'role_directory', 'role': name,
 2489	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 2490	            self._abort_round('ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED')
 2491	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED', name)
 2492	        try:
 2493	            # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** every
 2494	            # pipe: a pipe that is created after the deadline of the round
 2495	            # arrived is a pipe that nobody may close.
 2496	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stdout:' + name)
 2497	            stdout_read, stdout_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stdout')
 2498	            # The two ends are handed over to the core in one step: the
 2499	            # façade forgets them and the core registers them, so that
 2500	            # the two tables never name the same handle.
 2501	            self._adopt_from_api([(stdout_read, 'pipe_read'),
 2502	                                  (stdout_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
 2503	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stderr:' + name)
 2504	            stderr_read, stderr_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stderr')
 2505	            self._adopt_from_api([(stderr_read, 'pipe_read'),
 2506	                                  (stderr_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
 2507	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE_DONE:' + name)
 2508	        except BrokerError as error:
 2509	            record['failure'] = error.code
 2510	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_pipe', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
 2511	            self._abort_round('PIPE_FAILED')
 2512	            raise
 2513	        try:
 2514	            # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
 2515	            # creation: a role that is created after the deadline of the
 2516	            # round arrived would be a role that nobody may stop.
 2517	            self.check_startup_budget('CREATE:' + name)
 2518	            created = self.win32.create_process_suspended(
 2519	                executable=command['executable'], arguments=command['arguments'],
 2520	                working_directory=command['working_directory'],
 2521	                stdout_handle=stdout_write, stderr_handle=stderr_write,
 2522	                environment=command['environment'])
 2523	        except BrokerError as error:
 2524	            record['failure'] = error.code
 2525	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
 2526	                                  'code': error.code})
 2527	            # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
 2528	            # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
 2529	            # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
 2530	            retained = self._api_retained()
 2531	            if retained:
 2532	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
 2533	                                      'role': name, 'retained': retained})
 2534	            self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
 2535	            raise
 2536	        # The original handles of the process enter the pending table of
 2537	        # the round in the moment of their creation and the core takes
 2538	        # them right away: it is their owner from now on and it closes
 2539	        # them when it releases the records.
 2540	        #
 2541	        # The registration happens **before** the check of the shared
 2542	        # budget that follows the creation and before every other step
 2543	        # that may raise.  A process that was created after the deadline
 2544	        # arrived is only cleaned up and it is never owned by nobody:
 2545	        # the lease of the round may end, the ownership of the handles
 2546	        # may not.
 2547	        self._pending.add(created.process, 'process', role=name,
 2548	                          closeable=self._process_closeable(record))
 2549	        self._pending.add(created.thread, 'thread', role=name)
 2550	        self._pending.transfer(created.process, 'CORE')
 2551	        self._pending.transfer(created.thread, 'CORE')
 2552	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'process', 'owner': 'CORE'})
 2553	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'thread', 'owner': 'CORE'})
 2554	        record['created'] = True
 2555	        record['pid'] = created.pid
 2556	        record['creation_time'] = created.creation_time
 2557	        record['process_handle'] = created.process
 2558	        record['thread_handle'] = created.thread
 2559	        for failure in getattr(created, 'failures', ()):
 2560	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
 2561	                                  'code': failure})
 2562	        try:
 2563	            # The budget is checked again after the creation: a process
 2564	            # that was created after the deadline arrived is only
 2565	            # cleaned up, it is never assigned and it is never resumed.
 2566	            self.check_startup_budget('CREATED:' + name)
 2567	        except BrokerError as error:
 2568	            record['failure'] = error.code
 2569	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
 2570	                                  'code': error.code})
 2571	            # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
 2572	            # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
 2573	            # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
 2574	            retained = self._api_retained()
 2575	            if retained:
 2576	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
 2577	                                      'role': name, 'retained': retained})
 2578	            self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
 2579	            raise
 2580	        # The write ends belong to the child now; the broker keeps the read
 2581	        # ends only.  The copies of the broker are closed and they leave
 2582	        # the pending table: they are not pending any more.
 2583	        self._release_pending(stdout_write)
 2584	        self._release_pending(stderr_write)
 2585	        try:
 2586	            # The shared budget is checked before the assignment and
 2587	            # before the resume: a thread that is resumed after the
 2588	            # deadline of the round arrived is a service that runs
 2589	            # outside of the round.
 2590	            self.check_startup_budget('ASSIGN:' + name)
 2591	            self.win32.assign_process_to_job(self.job, created.process)
 2592	        except BrokerError as error:
 2593	            record['failure'] = error.code
 2594	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'assign', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
 2595	            # The process is still suspended: it never ran.  It is stopped
 2596	            # through its own original handle and its exit is confirmed.
 2597	            self._terminate_owned(created.process, record, 'ASSIGN_FAILED')
 2598	            self._abort_round('ASSIGN_FAILED')
 2599	            raise
 2600	        record['assigned'] = True
 2601	        try:
 2602	            self.check_startup_budget('RESUME:' + name)
 2603	            self.win32.resume_thread(created.thread)
 2604	        except BrokerError as error:
 2605	            record['failure'] = error.code
 2606	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'resume', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
 2607	            self._abort_round('RESUME_FAILED')
 2608	            raise
 2609	        record['resumed'] = True
 2610	        try:
 2611	            self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
 2612	            self._start_reader(record, stdout_read, 'stdout')
 2613	            self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
 2614	            self._start_reader(record, stderr_read, 'stderr')
 2615	        except BrokerError as error:
 2616	            record['failure'] = error.code
 2617	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
 2618	            self._abort_round('READER_FAILED')
 2619	            raise
 2620	
 2621	    def _release_pending(self, handle):
 2622	        """Release one pending handle that the child took over."""
 2623	        item = self._pending.take(handle)
 2624	        if item is None:
 2625	            return False
 2626	        try:
 2627	            self.win32.close_handle(handle)
 2628	        except BrokerError as error:
 2629	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2630	                                  'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
 2631	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
 2632	            return False
 2633	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 2634	            # A cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence: it is
 2635	            # named and it is raised, so that the cleanups that follow
 2636	            # it still run.
 2637	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2638	                                  'role': item['role'],
 2639	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
 2640	                                  'cancellation': True})
 2641	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
 2642	            raise
 2643	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2644	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
 2645	                                  'role': item['role'],
 2646	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 2647	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
 2648	            return False
 2649	        return True
 2650	
 2651	    def _prepare_directories(self, role):
 2652	        """Create the fresh directories of a role.
 2653	
 2654	        A directory that already exists is refused: a stale log, a stale
 2655	        pid lease or a stale sampler output of an earlier round may never
 2656	        be read as the output of this round.
 2657	
 2658	        The user directory of the ComfyUI role is the directory that the
 2659	        plan closed and not a fixed name: the round creates the
 2660	        directory that the command of the role really uses.
 2661	        """
 2662	        name = role['role']
 2663	        if name == 'COMFY':
 2664	            (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'service-os').mkdir(parents=True,
 2665	                                                                exist_ok=True)
 2666	            (Path(self.run_root) / role['data']['user_directory']).mkdir(
 2667	                parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 2668	        elif name == 'SAMPLER':
 2669	            (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler').mkdir(
 2670	                parents=True, exist_ok=False)
 2671	
 2672	    def _start_reader(self, record, handle, stream):
 2673	        """Hand the read end of a pipe over to its reader.
 2674	
 2675	        The reader is the owner of the read end from now on: the
 2676	        handover changes the owner of the handle and nothing else.  The
 2677	        handle stays in the pending table of the round until it is
 2678	        really closed, and the reader closes it when it is stopped.
 2679	
 2680	        The reader **object** is registered as the owner of the handle
 2681	        and not only its name: the pending pass asks the reader whether
 2682	        it is still alive before it closes the read end under it.
 2683	        """
 2684	        reader = _BoundedStreamReader(self.win32, handle, limit=MAX_STREAM_BYTES,
 2685	                                      name=record['role'] + '-' + stream)
 2686	        self.readers.append(reader)
 2687	        self._pending.transfer(handle, 'READER', owner_ref=reader)
 2688	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'pipe_read',
 2689	                                          'stream': stream, 'owner': 'READER'})
 2690	        reader.start()
 2691	
 2692	    @staticmethod
 2693	    def _process_closeable(record):
 2694	        """Whether the control handle of a process may be closed now.
 2695	
 2696	        The control handle of a process is closed **only** when the exit
 2697	        of that process was really confirmed.  The fact comes from the
 2698	        **record** of the process and it is not a name of a state: a
 2699	        process whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive and its
 2700	        control handle is the only handle that may stop it.
 2701	
 2702	        The same predicate is asked by the release of the records and by
 2703	        the pending pass of the round: the pending pass may not close
 2704	        behind the back of the owner of the handle.
 2705	        """
 2706	        return bool(record.get('exited'))
 2707	
 2708	    def _close_handle(self, kind, handle):
 2709	        """Close one owned handle and name a failure of the close.
 2710	
 2711	        A handle that was closed leaves the pending table of the round
 2712	        and it leaves the retained resources of the round: a handle that
 2713	        is really closed is never named as a resource any more.  A
 2714	        handle that could not be closed stays pending: it is a resource
 2715	        that this round still owns and it is named.
 2716	
 2717	        A **confirmed** close is notified through the **one** notification
 2718	        of the round: the job of the round, the records of the round, the
 2719	        reader that owns the handle and the retained resources of the
 2720	        round learn the very same fact, and every owner of a handle that
 2721	        was really closed knows that it must never close it again.
 2722	
 2723	        A **cancellation** in the middle of a close is never turned into
 2724	        an ordinary evidence: it is named and it is raised, so that the
 2725	        finalizer that follows it still runs and the cancellation
 2726	        continues to propagate after the cleanup.
 2727	        """
 2728	        entry = self._pending.find(handle)
 2729	        try:
 2730	            self.win32.close_handle(handle)
 2731	        except BrokerError as error:
 2732	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
 2733	                                  'code': error.code})
 2734	            return False
 2735	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 2736	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
 2737	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
 2738	                                  'cancellation': True})
 2739	            raise
 2740	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2741	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
 2742	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 2743	            return False
 2744	        self._pending.take(handle)
 2745	        # The handle is really closed: every owner of it is notified
 2746	        # through the one notification of the round.
 2747	        self._note_closed_handle(entry if entry is not None
 2748	                                 else {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind})
 2749	        return True
 2750	
 2751	    def _close_readers(self):
 2752	        """Stop the readers of the children and close their read handles.
 2753	
 2754	        The wait of a reader is bounded by the ONE cleanup deadline of the
 2755	        round and its state is recorded.  A reader that is still blocked
 2756	        when the wait is over is a *fact* of this round and it is
 2757	        reported as such: a daemon thread that is still running is not a
 2758	        finished thread, and the read end of such a reader is not closed
 2759	        under it.
 2760	
 2761	        Every reader is independent of the others: a reader that could
 2762	        not be joined never skips the coordination of the readers that
 2763	        follow it, and a reader whose thread never started is never
 2764	        joined at all.  A read end that was closed with a confirmed
 2765	        close is never closed a second time.
 2766	        """
 2767	        for reader in self.readers:
 2768	            try:
 2769	                self._close_one_reader(reader)
 2770	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 2771	                # A cancellation never skips the readers that follow it:
 2772	                # it is named and it is raised after every reader was
 2773	                # coordinated.
 2774	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
 2775	                                      'name': reader.name,
 2776	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__,
 2777	                                      'cancellation': True})
 2778	                self._readers_cancelled.append(error)
 2779	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2780	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
 2781	                                      'name': reader.name,
 2782	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 2783	        if self._readers_cancelled:
 2784	            raise self._readers_cancelled[0]
 2785	
 2786	    def _close_one_reader(self, reader):
 2787	        """Coordinate one reader of the round on its own.
 2788	
 2789	        The read end of a reader is closed **only** when the reader
 2790	        really stopped.  Every branch of the coordination asks the
 2791	        reader whether it is still alive: a wait that was cancelled and
 2792	        a join that failed are not a proof that the reader ended, and
 2793	        the read end of a live reader is never closed under it.
 2794	
 2795	        A cancellation in the middle of the coordination never skips the
 2796	        readers that follow it and it never skips the protection of the
 2797	        read end of the reader that is still alive.
 2798	        """
 2799	        if reader.handle is None:
 2800	            # The read end of this reader was closed with a confirmed
 2801	            # close already.
 2802	            return
 2803	        state = {'name': reader.name,
 2804	                 'finished': reader.finished.is_set(),
 2805	                 'joined': False,
 2806	                 'alive': reader.is_alive(),
 2807	                 'error': reader.error}
 2808	        if reader.ident is None and not reader.is_alive():
 2809	            # The thread of this reader never started.  A thread that
 2810	            # never started is never joined: the join of a thread that
 2811	            # was not started is an error of the caller and not a fact
 2812	            # of the round.  The handle of this reader is closed here,
 2813	            # because no thread of the round reads it.
 2814	            state['started'] = False
 2815	            state['alive'] = False
 2816	            state['finished'] = True
 2817	            self.reader_states.append(state)
 2818	            if self._close_handle('stream', reader.handle):
 2819	                reader.handle = None
 2820	            else:
 2821	                self._retain_resource('pipe_read', reader.handle,
 2822	                                      'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
 2823	            return
 2824	        state['started'] = True
 2825	        cancelled = None
 2826	        try:
 2827	            remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
 2828	            wait = min(READER_JOIN_SECONDS, remaining)
 2829	            if wait > 0:
 2830	                reader.finished.wait(wait)
 2831	            # The remaining budget is recomputed before the join: a wait
 2832	            # that already consumed part of the budget never receives the
 2833	            # whole budget again.
 2834	            remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
 2835	            reader.join(min(READER_JOIN_SECONDS, remaining))
 2836	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 2837	            # A cancellation never decides the fate of the read end: the
 2838	            # reader is asked again whether it is still alive.
 2839	            cancelled = error
 2840	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2841	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_reader',
 2842	                                  'name': reader.name,
 2843	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 2844	        alive = reader.is_alive()
 2845	        state['finished'] = reader.finished.is_set()
 2846	        state['joined'] = not alive
 2847	        state['alive'] = alive
 2848	        self.reader_states.append(state)
 2849	        if alive:
 2850	            # The reader still holds the read end.  The handle is not
 2851	            # closed under a running reader: it leaves the pending
 2852	            # table with its owner and the round keeps the fact.
 2853	            self._pending.take(reader.handle)
 2854	            self.reader_states[-1]['handle_retained'] = True
 2855	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader_not_stopped',
 2856	                                  'name': reader.name,
 2857	                                  'code': 'BROKER_READER_NOT_STOPPED'})
 2858	            if cancelled is not None:
 2859	                raise cancelled
 2860	            return
 2861	        if self._close_handle('stream', reader.handle):
 2862	            reader.handle = None
 2863	        else:
 2864	            self._retain_resource('pipe_read', reader.handle,
 2865	                                  'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
 2866	        if cancelled is not None:
 2867	            raise cancelled
 2868	
 2869	    def _terminate_owned(self, handle, record, reason):
 2870	        """Stop one owned process through its own original handle.
 2871	
 2872	        No pid is used and no name is used: only the handle that this core
 2873	        created is passed to the operating system.  The wait is bounded
 2874	        by the ONE cleanup deadline of the round: an expired deadline
 2875	        never starts a new wait.
 2876	        """
 2877	        try:
 2878	            self.win32.terminate_process(handle, 1)
 2879	            record['terminated'] = True
 2880	        except BrokerError as error:
 2881	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate', 'reason': reason,
 2882	                                  'code': error.code})
 2883	            return False
 2884	        timeout = self._wait_timeout_ms()
 2885	        if timeout <= 0:
 2886	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'reason': reason,
 2887	                                  'code': 'BROKER_CLEANUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'})
 2888	            return True
 2889	        try:
 2890	            record['exited'] = bool(self.win32.wait_process(handle, timeout))
 2891	        except BrokerError as error:
 2892	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'reason': reason, 'code': error.code})
 2893	        if record['exited']:
 2894	            try:
 2895	                record['exit_code'] = self.win32.exit_code(handle)
 2896	            except BrokerError as error:
 2897	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'reason': reason,
 2898	                                      'code': error.code})
 2899	        return True
 2900	
 2901	    # -- the stop of the round ---------------------------------------------
 2902	    def stop(self, reason='STOP', *, force_unknown=False, deadline=None):
 2903	        """Stop the round and release every handle that this core holds.
 2904	
 2905	        The stop is bounded and it is idempotent: a repeated stop does not
 2906	        terminate anything twice and it does not change the evidence of
 2907	        the round.  Every handle is reported on its own, so a handle that
 2908	        could not be closed stays visible and the round becomes unknown
 2909	        instead of a silent success.
 2910	
 2911	        The steps of the stop are **independent bounded finalizers**.  The
 2912	        termination of the job, the wait for the original processes, the
 2913	        stop of the readers, the query of the job, the release of the
 2914	        original handles, the release of the job, the release of the
 2915	        pending resources and the diagnosis of the round each run in
 2916	        their own guard: a failure of one of them never skips the others.
 2917	        The first failure of the round is the one that leaves the method
 2918	        and the later failures are named next to it.
 2919	
 2920	        A stop that did not release everything does **not** latch the
 2921	        round as released: the same owner may try the remaining work
 2922	        again, inside the ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  A handle
 2923	        that was closed with a confirmed close is never closed a second
 2924	        time.
 2925	        """
 2926	        if self._released:
 2927	            return self.evidence()
 2928	        if self.state == 'STOPPING' and self._cleanup_deadline_value is not None:
 2929	            # A stop that is already running: the owner of the round does
 2930	            # not start a second one.
 2931	            return self.evidence()
 2932	        self.state = 'STOPPING'
 2933	        if self.stop_reason is None:
 2934	            self.stop_reason = reason
 2935	        # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated here,
 2936	        # at the first stop, and every step below uses this very value.
 2937	        deadline = self.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
 2938	        primary = None
 2939	
 2940	        def step(stage, action):
 2941	            """Run one bounded finalizer of the stop.
 2942	
 2943	            A finalizer that fails never skips the finalizers that follow
 2944	            it.  A cancellation is named as a cancellation and it is
 2945	            never turned into an ordinary evidence: it becomes the
 2946	            primary failure of the round when nothing else failed, and
 2947	            it is raised after every finalizer was executed.
 2948	            """
 2949	            nonlocal primary
 2950	            try:
 2951	                action()
 2952	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the cleanup still runs
 2953	                cancelled = isinstance(error, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit))
 2954	                if primary is None:
 2955	                    primary = error
 2956	                self.failures.append({'stage': stage,
 2957	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__,
 2958	                                      'cancellation': cancelled})
 2959	
 2960	        step('diagnostic', self._collect_stream_counts)
 2961	        step('terminate_job', self._terminate_the_job)
 2962	        step('wait_processes', self._wait_the_processes)
 2963	        step('close_readers', self._close_readers)
 2964	        step('query_job', self._query_job)
 2965	        step('release_records', self._release_the_records)
 2966	        step('release_job', self._release_the_job)
 2967	        step('pending_resources', self._close_pending)
 2968	
 2969	        released = self._is_released()
 2970	        # The latch of a completed release is only set when the release
 2971	        # was really proven.  An unfinished cleanup never blocks the
 2972	        # recovery of the same owner.
 2973	        self._released = released
 2974	        self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED' if (force_unknown or not released) else 'RELEASED'
 2975	        if primary is not None:
 2976	            # The first failure of the round is not swallowed: the caller
 2977	            # sees it after the round was cleaned up.
 2978	            raise primary
 2979	        return self.evidence()
 2980	
 2981	    def _pending_handle_in_use(self, item):
 2982	        """Whether the owner of a pending handle is still using it.
 2983	
 2984	        The owner of a handle is an **object** and not a name: the
 2985	        reader that owns a read end is asked whether it is still alive.
 2986	        A read end that a live reader still reads from is never closed
 2987	        under it, whatever the branch that reached the pending pass did
 2988	        before.
 2989	        """
 2990	        owner = item.get('owner_ref')
 2991	        if owner is None:
 2992	            return False
 2993	        alive = getattr(owner, 'is_alive', None)
 2994	        if not callable(alive):
 2995	            return False
 2996	        try:
 2997	            return bool(alive())
 2998	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 2999	            # A reader that cannot answer is a reader that may still be
 3000	            # alive: the handle is protected.
 3001	            return True
 3002	
 3003	    def _close_pending(self):
 3004	        """Close the handles that are still pending and name the failures.
 3005	
 3006	        The pending pass is the **last** close of the round and it may
 3007	        close a handle that the release of the records or the release of
 3008	        the job could not close.  Every handle that it really closes is
 3009	        reported to the core: the job of the round, the records of the
 3010	        round and the retained resources of the round never name a
 3011	        handle that is closed.
 3012
