 4541	        released is not retained any more, and a worker whose handle of
 4542	        its thread could not be closed is retained again and it may be
 4543	        released by a later close.  A closed file never locks the
 4544	        recovery of the handle of a thread.
 4545	        """
 4546	        deadline = self._effective_deadline()
 4547	        alive = False
 4548	        retained = []
 4549	        for worker in (self._reader, self._writer):
 4550	            if worker is None:
 4551	                continue
 4552	            worker.stop_accepting()
 4553	            worker.request_cancel(deadline)
 4554	            if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
 4555	                # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner and the
 4556	                # file is never closed under it.
 4557	                alive = True
 4558	                retained.append(worker)
 4559	                continue
 4560	            # The thread of the worker really ended: the original handle
 4561	            # of the thread may be released now.  A handle that could
 4562	            # not be closed keeps its owner and a later close may try it
 4563	            # again.
 4564	            if not worker.release_thread_handle():
 4565	                retained.append(worker)
 4566	        # The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
 4567	        # worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
 4568	        # released is not retained any more.  A closed file never locks
 4569	        # the recovery of the handle of a thread.
 4570	        self._retained = retained
 4571	        self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
 4572	        self.facts['worker_alive'] = alive
 4573	        self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = all(
 4574	            worker.thread_handle_released
 4575	            for worker in (self._reader, self._writer) if worker is not None)
 4576	        if alive:
 4577	            # A live worker keeps the file: the file is never closed
 4578	            # under a worker that is still inside its operation.
 4579	            self.facts['closed'] = False
 4580	            return self.facts
 4581	        if self._closed:
 4582	            self.facts['closed'] = True
 4583	            self.facts['already_closed'] = True
 4584	            return self.facts
 4585	        try:
 4586	            self._file.close()
 4587	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 4588	            self.facts['closed'] = False
 4589	            self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
 4590	            return self.facts
 4591	        self._closed = True
 4592	        self.facts['closed'] = True
 4593	        return self.facts
 4594	
 4595	
 4596	def _make_endpoint(file_object, name, *, api=None, clock=None, context=None):
 4597	    """The endpoint of one end of the private pipe of this host.
 4598	
 4599	    The Windows host gets the supervised and cancellable endpoint and
 4600	    every other host gets the non blocking endpoint of the original
 4601	    descriptor.  The choice is made by the host and never by a string
 4602	    that a caller could set: a platform that is not Windows never loads
 4603	    the Windows table.
 4604	
 4605	    The Windows endpoint needs the primitive table of the host, because
 4606	    the cancellation of a synchronous operation needs the original
 4607	    handle of the thread that performs it.  The table is loaded only on
 4608	    Windows.
 4609	
 4610	    The ONE context of the round is handed to the endpoint: the
 4611	    endpoint reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it
 4612	    never keeps a budget of its own.
 4613	    """
 4614	    if os.name == 'nt':
 4615	        if api is None:
 4616	            api = WindowsApi()
 4617	        return _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, api=api, clock=clock,
 4618	                                    context=context)
 4619	    return _PosixPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, clock=clock)
 4620	
 4621	
 4622	# -- the private identity of a cancellation of the round --------------------
 4623	
 4624	class _CloseSource:
 4625	    """The private identity of one close of one channel.
 4626	
 4627	    The identity is an **object**: two closes of the same channel never
 4628	    share it and the owner of a cancellation is compared by identity
 4629	    and never by the name of a code.  The sequence is the order of the
 4630	    closes of the channel and it is part of the evidence.
 4631	    """
 4632	
 4633	    def __init__(self, sequence):
 4634	        self.sequence = sequence
 4635	        self.owner = 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'
 4636	
 4637	
 4638	class _ChannelCancel:
 4639	    """The cancel flag of one channel, with the owner that set it.
 4640	
 4641	    The flag names the **owner** that set it: a cancellation that the
 4642	    close of this channel initiated is provable and a cancellation that
 4643	    another owner initiated is not.  The owner is registered in the
 4644	    same atomic step as the setting of the flag and it is registered
 4645	    **before** the flag is set, so that a reader can never see the flag
 4646	    set without its owner.
 4647	
 4648	    The **first** owner wins: a flag that another owner already set is
 4649	    never re-labelled by a later one.  A cancellation that this channel
 4650	    did not initiate is therefore never taken for a cancellation of the
 4651	    close of this channel.
 4652	    """
 4653	
 4654	    def __init__(self):
 4655	        self._event = threading.Event()
 4656	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
 4657	        self._owner = None
 4658	
 4659	    def is_set(self):
 4660	        return self._event.is_set()
 4661	
 4662	    def set(self, owner=None):
 4663	        """Set the flag and name the owner that set it.
 4664	
 4665	        The owner is written under the lock of the flag and it is
 4666	        written **before** the flag is set: the registration of the
 4667	        owner is never later than the cancellation itself.
 4668	        """
 4669	        with self._lock:
 4670	            if not self._event.is_set():
 4671	                if owner is not None:
 4672	                    self._owner = owner
 4673	                self._event.set()
 4674	
 4675	    def owner(self):
 4676	        """The owner that set the flag, or None when it named none."""
 4677	        with self._lock:
 4678	            return self._owner
 4679	
 4680	    def wait(self, timeout=None):
 4681	        return self._event.wait(timeout)
 4682	
 4683	
 4684	def _cancel_source_error(error, cancel):
 4685	    """Stamp the owner of a cancel flag on the error of a cancellation.
 4686	
 4687	    The owner is read from the flag **at the origin of the error**: the
 4688	    error itself carries the identity of the owner that really set the
 4689	    flag and it never carries a claim of a caller.  A flag that names
 4690	    no owner leaves the error untouched: an unknown source is never
 4691	    taken for a known one.
 4692	    """
 4693	    owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
 4694	    if not callable(owner):
 4695	        return error
 4696	    try:
 4697	        source = owner()
 4698	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 4699	        return error
 4700	    if source is None:
 4701	        return error
 4702	    try:
 4703	        error.lvw_cancel_source = source
 4704	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 4705	        pass
 4706	    return error
 4707	
 4708	
 4709	# -- the private control channel -------------------------------------------
 4710	
 4711	class StreamChannel:
 4712	    """The private control channel of the broker.
 4713	
 4714	    The channel is the broker's own standard input and output.  It is
 4715	    bounded three times: one frame is at most 32 KiB, the channel carries
 4716	    at most 1024 frames and the text of the children is bounded by the
 4717	    core.
 4718	
 4719	    The read side is drained by one background thread, so the broker never
 4720	    blocks on the read.  A parent that stops talking therefore cannot hold
 4721	    the deadline of the round: the deadline is checked by the broker
 4722	    itself.
 4723	
 4724	    The channel owns the original handles of its two ends.  A read and a
 4725	    write are bounded by an absolute deadline and by a cancel flag, so a
 4726	    peer that never reads can never hold the supervisor of the round.
 4727	    """
 4728	
 4729	    def __init__(self, reader, writer, *, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES,
 4730	                 max_frame_bytes=MAX_FRAME_BYTES, name='channel',
 4731	                 read_endpoint=None, write_endpoint=None, clock=None, api=None,
 4732	                 context=None, custody=None):
 4733	        self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=max_frames)
 4734	        self._max_frames = max_frames
 4735	        self._max_frame_bytes = max_frame_bytes
 4736	        self._count = 0
 4737	        self._overflow = False
 4738	        self._overflow_facts = {}
 4739	        self._eof = False
 4740	        self._error = None
 4741	        self._buffer = b''
 4742	        self._deadline = None
 4743	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
 4744	        # The cancel flag of the channel names the owner that set it:
 4745	        # only a cancellation that the close of **this** channel
 4746	        # initiated is provable and only a provable one is a normal
 4747	        # fact of the close.
 4748	        self._cancel = _ChannelCancel()
 4749	        # The private state of the close of this channel: the identity
 4750	        # of the close that is running now and the named fact of the
 4751	        # cancellation that this close really initiated.
 4752	        self._close_source = None
 4753	        self._close_sequence = 0
 4754	        self._close_cancellation = None
 4755	        # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
 4756	        # failure of the reader: it is a parallel fact and it never
 4757	        # replaces the failure.
 4758	        self._parallel_cancellation = None
 4759	        self.reader_alive = None
 4760	        self.reader_finished = None
 4761	        # The ONE context of the round.  The channel and its endpoints
 4762	        # read the absolute deadline of the round from it and they never
 4763	        # keep a budget of their own.
 4764	        self._context = context
 4765	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drain, daemon=True,
 4766	                                        name='lvw-native-custody-channel')
 4767	        # The channel registers itself with the custody of the launcher
 4768	        # **before** it builds anything: a channel whose construction
 4769	        # failed half way - an endpoint that could not be built, a
 4770	        # reader thread that did not start - may not leave an endpoint
 4771	        # and a thread without an owner.  The outer owner of the launch
 4772	        # finds the partial channel through this registration and it
 4773	        # never closes the files of the process by itself.
 4774	        self._custody = custody
 4775	        if custody is not None:
 4776	            custody.retain('channel', self, 'constructing')
 4777	        try:
 4778	            self._reader = read_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
 4779	                reader, name + '-read', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
 4780	            self._writer = writer_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
 4781	                writer, name + '-write', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
 4782	            self._thread.start()
 4783	        except BaseException:
 4784	            # The construction of the channel did not return: the
 4785	            # channel keeps its owner in the custody of the launcher and
 4786	            # it is never forgotten.
 4787	            raise
 4788	
 4789	    def bind_context(self, context):
 4790	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to the channel.
 4791	
 4792	        The context is bound to the channel **and** to its two
 4793	        endpoints: every layer of the round reads the same absolute
 4794	        deadline and none of them keeps a deadline of its own.
 4795	        """
 4796	        if context is not None:
 4797	            self._context = context
 4798	            for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
 4799	                binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_context', None)
 4800	                if callable(binder):
 4801	                    binder(context)
 4802	        return self._context
 4803	
 4804	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
 4805	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the channel.
 4806	
 4807	        The deadline is bound to the channel **and** to its two
 4808	        endpoints.  A binding that happens after a read started still
 4809	        tightens that read: the deadline is a property of the endpoint
 4810	        and not a local argument of one call.
 4811	        """
 4812	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 4813	        if value is None:
 4814	            return self._deadline
 4815	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
 4816	            return self._deadline
 4817	        self._deadline = value
 4818	        for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
 4819	            binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_deadline', None)
 4820	            if callable(binder):
 4821	                try:
 4822	                    binder(value)
 4823	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 4824	                    pass
 4825	        return value
 4826	
 4827	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
 4828	        """The ONE deadline that a call of this channel really uses.
 4829	
 4830	        The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
 4831	        channel, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
 4832	        cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of the
 4833	        four.
 4834	
 4835	        The published cleanup deadline is part of it.  A round that
 4836	        entered its cleanup publishes one deadline, and the transport of
 4837	        the round - the write of the terminal reply and the close of the
 4838	        channel - is bound by it: a transport that still waited with the
 4839	        long lease of the round would outlive the only cleanup budget
 4840	        of the round.
 4841	        """
 4842	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 4843	        bound = self._deadline
 4844	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
 4845	            value = bound
 4846	        context = self._context
 4847	        if context is not None:
 4848	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
 4849	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
 4850	                value = absolute
 4851	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
 4852	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
 4853	                value = cleanup
 4854	        return value
 4855	
 4856	    def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
 4857	        """The seconds that are left until the deadline of the channel."""
 4858	        value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
 4859	        if value is None:
 4860	            return None
 4861	        return max(0.0, value - (self._clock or time.monotonic)())
 4862	
 4863	    def _drain(self):
 4864	        try:
 4865	            while True:
 4866	                block = self._reader.read(self._deadline, self._cancel)
 4867	                if not block:
 4868	                    break
 4869	                buffer = self._buffer + block
 4870	                self._buffer = b''
 4871	                while True:
 4872	                    index = buffer.find(b'\n')
 4873	                    if index < 0:
 4874	                        break
 4875	                    raw = buffer[:index + 1]
 4876	                    buffer = buffer[index + 1:]
 4877	                    if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
 4878	                        self._record_overflow('FRAME_TOO_LARGE',
 4879	                                              {'frame_bytes': len(raw),
 4880	                                               'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
 4881	                        return
 4882	                    self._count += 1
 4883	                    if self._count > self._max_frames:
 4884	                        self._record_overflow('TOO_MANY_FRAMES',
 4885	                                              {'frames': self._count,
 4886	                                               'limit': self._max_frames})
 4887	                        return
 4888	                    while True:
 4889	                        self._check_wait()
 4890	                        try:
 4891	                            self._queue.put(raw, timeout=0.5)
 4892	                            break
 4893	                        except queue.Full:
 4894	                            if self._eof:
 4895	                                return
 4896	                            continue
 4897	                # The remainder of the block has no newline.  The buffer
 4898	                # of the channel is bounded: it is checked on **every**
 4899	                # append and an oversize buffer is refused at once.  One
 4900	                # byte beyond the limit is kept, so that the overflow is
 4901	                # detected and not silently trimmed.
 4902	                if len(buffer) > self._max_frame_bytes:
 4903	                    self._buffer = buffer[:self._max_frame_bytes + 1]
 4904	                    self._record_overflow('BUFFER_TOO_LARGE',
 4905	                                          {'buffer_bytes': len(self._buffer),
 4906	                                           'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
 4907	                    return
 4908	                self._buffer = buffer
 4909	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 4910	            self._record_reader_error(error)
 4911	        finally:
 4912	            self._eof = True
 4913	            if self._buffer and self._error is None:
 4914	                # The stream ended in the middle of a frame.  An
 4915	                # incomplete frame is a named failure of the channel and
 4916	                # it is never dropped silently.
 4917	                self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME'
 4918	
 4919	    def _record_overflow(self, reason, facts):
 4920	        """Record the overflow of the channel at the place where it happened.
 4921	
 4922	        The overflow is a **named** failure of the channel and it is
 4923	        recorded **here**, where it really happened.  A flag that only
 4924	        the internals of the channel know would leave the close of the
 4925	        channel with no error at all: the reader of a channel that
 4926	        overflowed would look like a reader that ended normally and the
 4927	        round would release its resources on a fact that never happened.
 4928	
 4929	        The error of the channel is **sticky**: the first named failure
 4930	        of the reader stays the error of the channel and the overflow
 4931	        never replaces it.
 4932	        """
 4933	        self._overflow = True
 4934	        self._overflow_facts = dict(facts, reason=reason)
 4935	        if self._error is None:
 4936	            self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW'
 4937	
 4938	    def _record_reader_error(self, error):
 4939	        """Record the error of the reader of this channel.
 4940	
 4941	        A cancellation that **this** channel initiated with **this**
 4942	        close is not a failure of the reader: it is a named fact of the
 4943	        close.  It is kept with its code, with its source and with its
 4944	        order for the evidence, and it never replaces the error of the
 4945	        reader and it never clears a historical error of the reader.
 4946	
 4947	        Every other error keeps the original error of the reader and the
 4948	        round stays unknown: an ordinary failure of the read, a timeout,
 4949	        an overflow, a partial frame, an error that was already there
 4950	        before the close and a cancellation whose owner is unknown or
 4951	        is not the close of this channel.
 4952	        """
 4953	        source = getattr(error, 'lvw_cancel_source', None)
 4954	        if source is not None and source is self._close_source:
 4955	            # This cancellation was initiated by the close of this
 4956	            # channel: it is a named fact of the close and it is not a
 4957	            # failure of the reader.  The historical error of the reader
 4958	            # is never cleared and it is never replaced by this fact.
 4959	            self._close_cancellation = {
 4960	                'code': getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__,
 4961	                'source': getattr(source, 'owner', 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'),
 4962	                'sequence': getattr(source, 'sequence', None),
 4963	                'error_type': type(error).__name__,
 4964	            }
 4965	            return
 4966	        parallel = getattr(error, 'lvw_parallel_cancellation', None)
 4967	        if parallel is not None:
 4968	            # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
 4969	            # failure of the reader is a **parallel** fact: it is named
 4970	            # next to the failure and it never replaces it.
 4971	            self._parallel_cancellation = dict(parallel)
 4972	        self._error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
 4973	
 4974	    def _check_wait(self):
 4975	        """The cancel flag and the deadline of the wait of the reader."""
 4976	        if self._cancel.is_set():
 4977	            # The error carries the owner of the flag: the reader of
 4978	            # the channel and the close of the channel agree on the
 4979	            # origin of the cancellation and the evidence can prove it.
 4980	            raise _cancel_source_error(BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_CANCELLED'),
 4981	                                       self._cancel)
 4982	        remaining = self._remaining()
 4983	        if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
 4984	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT')
 4985	
 4986	    def read(self, timeout):
 4987	        """One raw frame, ``None`` when nothing arrived, ``b''`` at the end."""
 4988	        if self._overflow:
 4989	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW')
 4990	        try:
 4991	            return self._queue.get(timeout=max(0.0, timeout))
 4992	        except queue.Empty:
 4993	            if self._error:
 4994	                raise BrokerError(self._error)
 4995	            if self._eof:
 4996	                return b''
 4997	            return None
 4998	
 4999	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
 5000	        """Write one frame, bounded by the deadline of the round.
 5001	
 5002	        The number of the bytes that really went into the pipe is
 5003	        checked: a partial write is never reported as a success.
 5004	        """
 5005	        if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
 5006	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
 5007	        target = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
 5008	        written = self._writer.write(raw, target, self._cancel)
 5009	        if written is not None and written != len(raw):
 5010	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_WRITE_INCOMPLETE',
 5011	                              'written=%s:expected=%d' % (written, len(raw)))
 5012	        return written
 5013	
 5014	    def close(self):
 5015	        """Close the channel and report whether the reader really stopped.
 5016	
 5017	        The reader thread is joined with a bounded wait.  A reader that
 5018	        did not stop is reported as such: a daemon thread is not a
 5019	        finished thread, so the fact that the reader is still alive is
 5020	        kept visible instead of being hidden behind the close.  The read
 5021	        end is closed by its owner only when the reader really stopped.
 5022	
 5023	        The facts of the close carry the error of the reader, the number
 5024	        of the workers that the endpoints retained and the fact that the
 5025	        original handles of those workers were released: a reader that
 5026	        ended with an error is not a successful end.
 5027	
 5028	        The close may be **repeated**: a close that did not confirm its
 5029	        ends may try them again, and a close of an endpoint that is
 5030	        already closed keeps the fact that it is closed.
 5031	
 5032	        The error of the reader of the channel is **sticky** and it is
 5033	        never cleared by a later close of the channel: a close that
 5034	        returned is not a release and the error of the reader stays.
 5035	
 5036	        The close **initiates** the cancellation of the reader and it
 5037	        names itself as the owner of that cancellation before the flag
 5038	        is set.  A cancellation that this close really initiated is a
 5039	        named fact of the close and it is not a failure of the reader; a
 5040	        cancellation of another owner, a failure of the read that
 5041	        happened before the close and every other error of the reader
 5042	        keep the round unknown.
 5043	        """
 5044	        facts = {}
 5045	        # The identity of this close is published **before** the
 5046	        # cancellation is initiated and the flag registers the owner in
 5047	        # the same atomic step as the setting of the flag.
 5048	        self._close_sequence += 1
 5049	        source = _CloseSource(self._close_sequence)
 5050	        self._close_source = source
 5051	        self._cancel.set(source)
 5052	        try:
 5053	            write_facts = self._writer.close()
 5054	            facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(write_facts.get('closed'))
 5055	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = int(
 5056	                write_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
 5057	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
 5058	                write_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
 5059	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5060	            facts['write_end_closed'] = False
 5061	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = 0
 5062	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
 5063	        remaining = self._remaining()
 5064	        join_seconds = CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS if remaining is None else remaining
 5065	        try:
 5066	            self._thread.join(join_seconds)
 5067	        except RuntimeError:
 5068	            # The thread of the reader never started: it is not a thread
 5069	            # of the round that ended and it is never joined.  No thread
 5070	            # reads the read end of the channel and the read end may be
 5071	            # closed.
 5072	            self.reader_alive = False
 5073	            self.reader_finished = True
 5074	        else:
 5075	            self.reader_alive = self._thread.is_alive()
 5076	            self.reader_finished = not self.reader_alive
 5077	        facts['reader_finished'] = self.reader_finished
 5078	        facts['reader_error'] = self._error
 5079	        # The facts of the overflow are published with the close: the
 5080	        # overflow is a named failure of the channel and it is never
 5081	        # only an internal flag.
 5082	        facts['overflow'] = bool(self._overflow)
 5083	        facts['overflow_facts'] = dict(self._overflow_facts)
 5084	        # The parallel fact of the cancellation of the close: it never
 5085	        # replaces the error of the reader.
 5086	        facts['reader_parallel_cancellation'] = (
 5087	            dict(self._parallel_cancellation)
 5088	            if self._parallel_cancellation else None)
 5089	        # The named fact of the cancellation that this close really
 5090	        # initiated.  It is **not** the error of the reader and it does
 5091	        # not prove that the reader, the thread, the worker or the
 5092	        # handle of the round were released: the strict facts of the
 5093	        # close above are the ones that prove it.
 5094	        facts['close_cancellation'] = (dict(self._close_cancellation)
 5095	                                       if self._close_cancellation else None)
 5096	        if self.reader_alive:
 5097	            # The reader is still inside the pipe: the read end is not
 5098	            # closed under it.  It stays owned by the channel and the
 5099	            # round keeps the fact.  The facts of the reader endpoint are
 5100	            # the real ones and they are never replaced by a zero.
 5101	            endpoint_facts = getattr(self._reader, 'facts', None)
 5102	            endpoint_facts = endpoint_facts if isinstance(endpoint_facts, dict) else {}
 5103	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
 5104	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
 5105	                endpoint_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
 5106	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
 5107	                endpoint_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
 5108	            facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
 5109	                facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
 5110	                and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
 5111	            facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
 5112	                                                  + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
 5113	            return facts
 5114	        try:
 5115	            read_facts = self._reader.close()
 5116	            facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(read_facts.get('closed'))
 5117	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
 5118	                read_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
 5119	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
 5120	                read_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
 5121	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5122	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
 5123	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = 0
 5124	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
 5125	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
 5126	            facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
 5127	            and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
 5128	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
 5129	                                              + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
 5130	        return facts
 5131	
 5132	    @property
 5133	    def eof(self):
 5134	        return self._eof
 5135	
 5136	    @property
 5137	    def overflow(self):
 5138	        return self._overflow
 5139	
 5140	    @property
 5141	    def frames(self):
 5142	        return self._count
 5143	
 5144	
 5145	# -- the bounded command loop ----------------------------------------------
 5146	
 5147	class _StartupOwner:
 5148	    """The bounded owner of one startup step of the round.
 5149	
 5150	    A startup step may block: the creation of a job, the creation of a
 5151	    process and the assignment of a process may all block inside the
 5152	    operating system.  Such a step may not run unbounded inside the one
 5153	    supervision thread of the round.
 5154	
 5155	    The owner therefore runs the step in its own thread and hands the
 5156	    result over through a **bounded** channel of one slot.  The
 5157	    supervisor waits for the result until the absolute deadline of the
 5158	    round.  A step that did not finish in time keeps the round unknown,
 5159	    keeps the thread and its owner, and a result that arrives late may
 5160	    only clean up: it may never continue the startup of the round.
 5161	    """
 5162	
 5163	    def __init__(self, *, name, clock, deadline):
 5164	        self.name = name
 5165	        self._clock = clock
 5166	        self._deadline = deadline
 5167	        self._results = queue.Queue(maxsize=STARTUP_INFLIGHT_SLOTS)
 5168	        self._thread = None
 5169	        self._error = None
 5170	        self._value = None
 5171	        self._finished = threading.Event()
 5172	        self._expired = False
 5173	
 5174	    def start(self, action):
 5175	        """Run one startup step in the thread of this owner."""
 5176	        self._thread = threading.Thread(
 5177	            target=self._run, args=(action,), daemon=True,
 5178	            name='lvw-native-custody-startup-' + self.name)
 5179	        self._thread.start()
 5180	        return self
 5181	
 5182	    def _run(self, action):
 5183	        try:
 5184	            value = action()
 5185	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5186	            self._error = error
 5187	            outcome = ('error', error)
 5188	        else:
 5189	            self._value = value
 5190	            outcome = ('value', value)
 5191	        # The **attempt** of the step ended.  The event of the attempt is
 5192	        # published here and it is never a proof that the thread of the
 5193	        # step ended: the thread still has to publish its result and to
 5194	        # leave.
 5195	        self._finished.set()
 5196	        self._put(outcome)
 5197	
 5198	    def _put(self, item):
 5199	        try:
 5200	            self._results.put_nowait(item)
 5201	        except queue.Full:
 5202	            # The bounded channel is full: the result is dropped and the
 5203	            # owner keeps the error.  The channel never grows.
 5204	            pass
 5205	
 5206	    def await_result(self):
 5207	        """Wait for the result of the step, bounded by the deadline."""
 5208	        while True:
 5209	            if self._clock() >= self._deadline:
 5210	                self._expired = True
 5211	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', self.name)
 5212	            try:
 5213	                kind, value = self._results.get(timeout=POLL_SLICE_SECONDS)
 5214	            except queue.Empty:
 5215	                continue
 5216	            if kind == 'error':
 5217	                raise value
 5218	            return value
 5219	
 5220	    def settle(self, seconds):
 5221	        """Wait, bounded, for the **thread** of this owner to end.
 5222	
 5223	        The wait is bounded by the shared cleanup deadline of the round
 5224	        and it never adds a settle budget of its own.
 5225	
 5226	        The end of the **attempt** of the step and the end of the
 5227	        **thread** that performs it are two different facts.  The event
 5228	        of the attempt is published before the result and before the
 5229	        thread leaves, and it is therefore never a proof that the
 5230	        thread ended.  The settle asks the thread itself: a thread that
 5231	        is still running keeps its owner and the round stays unknown.
 5232	        """
 5233	        if self._thread is None:
 5234	            return True
 5235	        try:
 5236	            self._thread.join(max(0.0, seconds))
 5237	        except RuntimeError:
 5238	            # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
 5239	            # round that ended.
 5240	            return False
 5241	        return not self._thread.is_alive()
 5242	
 5243	    @property
 5244	    def expired(self):
 5245	        """Whether the step of this owner missed the deadline."""
 5246	        return self._expired
 5247	
 5248	    @property
 5249	    def in_flight(self):
 5250	        """Whether the step of this owner is still running."""
 5251	        return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive()
 5252	
 5253	
 5254	class BrokerLoop:
 5255	    """The bounded command loop of the broker.
 5256	
 5257	    The loop owns the absolute deadline of the round.  It is fixed once,
 5258	    from the lease of the plan, and it is never moved: neither a status
 5259	    request nor a heartbeat may extend it.
 5260	
 5261	    The round is stopped when the parent asks for it, when the channel
 5262	    reaches its end, when a bound of the channel or of the text of the
 5263	    children is exceeded or when the absolute deadline arrives.  A stop
 5264	    that was not requested by the parent keeps the evidence of the round
 5265	    unknown: the round did not end in a proven release.
 5266	
 5267	    The startup of the round is supervised.  The arm and the launch are
 5268	    **bounded startup steps** with their own owner: a step that blocks
 5269	    may not hold the supervision of the round and a step that returns
 5270	    after the absolute deadline may only clean up.
 5271	    """
 5272	
 5273	    def __init__(self, *, core, transport, plan, nonce, clock=None, wall=None,
 5274	                 max_frames=MAX_FRAMES):
 5275	        self.core = core
 5276	        self.transport = transport
 5277	        self.plan = plan
 5278	        self.nonce = nonce
 5279	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
 5280	        self._wall = wall or time.time
 5281	        self._max_frames = max_frames
 5282	        self._sequence = 0
 5283	        self._last_sequence = None
 5284	        self._frames = 0
 5285	        self._sent_frames = 0
 5286	        self._deadline_wall = None
 5287	        self._deadline_mono = None
 5288	        self._last_frame_wall = None
 5289	        self._last_alive_wall = None
 5290	        self._stopped = False
 5291	        # The ONE context of the round.  The loop holds the very same
 5292	        # object as the core: the deadlines, the cancel and the unknown
 5293	        # of the round are one state and not one number per layer.
 5294	        self.context = core.context
 5295	        # The owner of the startup step that is in flight.  A step that
 5296	        # did not end keeps the round unknown and it keeps its owner.
 5297	        self._startup_owner = None
 5298	        self._startup_in_flight = None
 5299	
 5300	    # -- the deadline ------------------------------------------------------
 5301	    def _fix_deadline(self):
 5302	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round, once, before anything.
 5303	
 5304	        The deadline is fixed before the job of the round is created,
 5305	        before the first reply is written and before the first role is
 5306	        launched.  It is the earlier of the lease of the plan and the
 5307	        runtime of the plan and it is never moved afterwards.
 5308	        """
 5309	        if self._deadline_mono is not None:
 5310	            return self._deadline_mono
 5311	        wall_now = self._wall()
 5312	        self._deadline_wall = min(self.plan.lease_expires_at,
 5313	                                  wall_now + self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
 5314	        self._deadline_mono = self._clock() + max(0.0, self._deadline_wall - wall_now)
 5315	        return self._deadline_mono
 5316	
 5317	    def remaining_seconds(self):
 5318	        if self._deadline_mono is None:
 5319	            return float(self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
 5320	        return max(0.0, self._deadline_mono - self._clock())
 5321	
 5322	    # -- the replies -------------------------------------------------------
 5323	    def _check_frame_budget(self, reserved=RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES):
 5324	        """The frame budget of the channel, in both directions.
 5325	
 5326	        The budget of the round is shared by the frames that the broker
 5327	        sends and by the frames that it receives: the round of six hours
 5328	        with its heartbeat stays inside the budget of the channel.  The
 5329	        frames of the terminal exchange are reserved and the budget is
 5330	        never widened and never renewed.
 5331	
 5332	        The check is the **total** of both directions.  A single
 5333	        direction is never a substitute for the total: a channel that
 5334	        received a thousand frames and sent none is not inside the
 5335	        budget when the budget is a thousand and twenty four frames.
 5336	        """
 5337	        if self._frames + self._sent_frames + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
 5338	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED',
 5339	                              'sent=%d:received=%d' % (self._sent_frames,
 5340	                                                       self._frames))
 5341	
 5342	    def _reply(self, kind, state, detail, *, terminal=False):
 5343	        self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
 5344	        self._sent_frames += 1
 5345	        self._sequence += 1
 5346	        frame = {'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA, 'kind': kind, 'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
 5347	                 'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
 5348	                 'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
 5349	                 'nonce': self.nonce, 'sequence': self._sequence,
 5350	                 'state': state, 'detail': detail}
 5351	        raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
 5352	                         separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
 5353	        if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
 5354	            frame['detail'] = {'truncated': True}
 5355	            raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
 5356	                             separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
 5357	        # The deadline of the round is already bound to the transport, so
 5358	        # the write of the frame is bounded by it.  The transport is
 5359	        # never called with an argument that it does not know.
 5360	        self.transport.write(raw + b'\n')
 5361	
 5362	    # -- the supervised startup --------------------------------------------
 5363	    def _guarded_startup(self, action, stage):
 5364	        """One startup step, guarded by the shared deadline.
 5365	
 5366	        The shared budget is checked **before** the step and **after**
 5367	        it returned.  A step that starts after the deadline of the round
 5368	        arrived never runs at all, and a step that finished after the
 5369	        deadline may only clean up: it may never continue the startup of
 5370	        the round and it may never write a successful reply.
 5371	
 5372	        The supervision publishes the cancel of the round when the
 5373	        deadline arrived: the core and every later step of the startup
 5374	        see it and they stop.
 5375	        """
 5376	        def guarded():
 5377	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
 5378	            action()
 5379	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
 5380	            return None
 5381	        return guarded
 5382	
 5383	    def _check_startup_budget(self, stage):
 5384	        """Check the shared budget of the startup of the round."""
 5385	        try:
 5386	            self.core.check_startup_budget(stage)
 5387	        except BrokerError:
 5388	            # The supervision publishes the cancel of the round: the
 5389	            # core and every later step of the startup see it.
 5390	            self.context.request_cancel()
 5391	            self._abort_started_round()
 5392	            raise
 5393	
 5394	    def _abort_started_round(self):
 5395	        """Release what the round acquired, bounded by the shared budget."""
 5396	        try:
 5397	            self.core.stop('STARTUP_AFTER_DEADLINE', force_unknown=True)
 5398	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5399	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'startup_abort',
 5400	                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 5401	
 5402	    def _run_startup(self, stage, action):
 5403	        """Run one startup step under the absolute deadline of the round.
 5404	
 5405	        **Every** exit of this method waits for the **real** exit of the
 5406	        thread of the step: the success, the timeout, the ordinary
 5407	        failure, the cancellation and the ``SystemExit``.  The attempt
 5408	        of the step and the thread that performs it are two different
 5409	        facts and the owner of the step is released only after the real
 5410	        exit of its thread.
 5411	
 5412	        A thread that did not exit keeps its owner: it stays in the
 5413	        owners that are in flight and the round keeps the fact.  It is
 5414	        never released because the attempt ended and it is never given a
 5415	        new budget.
 5416	        """
 5417	        owner = _StartupOwner(name=stage, clock=self._clock,
 5418	                              deadline=self._deadline_mono)
 5419	        self._startup_owner = owner
 5420	        self.context.register_inflight(owner)
 5421	        owner.start(self._guarded_startup(action, stage))
 5422	        # The budget of the settle is the **absolute** deadline of the
 5423	        # round.  It is computed before the step is awaited and it never
 5424	        # publishes the cleanup deadline of the round: a round that did
 5425	        # not fail keeps its whole budget.
 5426	        settle_budget = self._startup_settle_seconds()
 5427	        try:
 5428	            value = owner.await_result()
 5429	        except BrokerError as error:
 5430	            if error.code not in ('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED',
 5431	                                  'BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED'):
 5432	                # An ordinary failure of the step: the thread of the
 5433	                # step is still coordinated in the finally below and the
 5434	                # original failure is the one that leaves this method.
 5435	                raise
 5436	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage, 'code': error.code})
 5437	            # The step did not finish before the deadline.  The owner
 5438	            # keeps its thread: a late result may only clean up.  The
 5439	            # wait for the end of the step is bounded by the **shared**
 5440	            # cleanup deadline of the round and it never adds a settle
 5441	            # budget of its own.
 5442	            settle_budget = self.context.remaining_cleanup()
 5443	            raise
 5444	        finally:
 5445	            # The real exit of the thread is asked on **every** exit.
 5446	            try:
 5447	                settled = owner.settle(settle_budget)
 5448	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5449	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
 5450	                                           'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 5451	                settled = False
 5452	            if not settled:
 5453	                self._startup_in_flight = owner
 5454	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
 5455	                                           'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
 5456	            else:
 5457	                self.context.release_inflight(owner)
 5458	        return value
 5459	
 5460	    def _startup_settle_seconds(self):
 5461	        """The bounded wait for the real exit of a startup thread.
 5462	
 5463	        The wait is bounded by the **remaining** of the absolute
 5464	        deadline of the round and by the settle bound of the package.
 5465	        It never publishes the cleanup deadline of the round - the
 5466	        cleanup of a round that did not fail keeps its whole budget -
 5467	        and it never opens a budget of its own.
 5468	        """
 5469	        remaining = self.context.remaining_absolute()
 5470	        if remaining is None:
 5471	            return STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS
 5472	        return max(POLL_SLICE_SECONDS, min(remaining, STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS))
 5473	
 5474	    # -- the run -----------------------------------------------------------
 5475	    def run(self):
 5476	        """Run the round and always release what the round owns.
 5477	
 5478	        The loop has a ``finally`` that does not depend on the diagnosis
 5479	        of the round and not on the success of a write: a round that
 5480	        could not write its first reply still releases the job and the
 5481	        handles that it created.
 5482	        """
 5483	        self._fix_deadline()
 5484	        # The core and the loop read the same clock: the deadlines of
 5485	        # the round and the ONE cleanup deadline are in one domain.
 5486	        binder_clock = getattr(self.core, 'bind_clock', None)
 5487	        if callable(binder_clock):
 5488	            binder_clock(self._clock)
 5489	        self.core.bind_absolute_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
 5490	        binder = getattr(self.transport, 'bind_deadline', None)
 5491	        if callable(binder):
 5492	            # The transport of the channel is bound to the deadline of
 5493	            # the round before the job of the round exists: a read or a
 5494	            # write of the channel can never outlive the round.
 5495	            binder(self._deadline_mono)
 5496	        try:
 5497	            try:
 5498	                self._run_startup('ARM', self.core.arm)
 5499	            except BrokerError as error:
 5500	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
 5501	                            terminal=True)
 5502	                self._write_receipt('ARM_FAILED')
 5503	                return 3
 5504	            self._reply('READY', self.core.state, {'broker': self.core.broker_identity})
 5505	            try:
 5506	                self._run_startup('LAUNCH', self.core.launch)
 5507	            except BrokerError as error:
 5508	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
 5509	                            terminal=True)
 5510	                self._write_receipt('LAUNCH_FAILED')
 5511	                return 3
 5512	            self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
 5513	            exit_code = self._loop()
 5514	            self._write_receipt(self.core.stop_reason)
 5515	            return exit_code
 5516	        finally:
 5517	            self._ensure_stopped()
 5518	
 5519	    def _ensure_stopped(self):
 5520	        """Release the resources of the round, whatever happened before.
 5521	
 5522	        The release never raises: a failure of the release is kept by the
 5523	        core and it never replaces the first failure of the round.
 5524	        """
 5525	        if self._stopped:
 5526	            return
 5527	        self._stopped = True
 5528	        if self._startup_in_flight is not None:
 5529	            # A startup step is still running: the core belongs to the
 5530	            # in-flight owner and the round keeps the fact.  The cleanup
 5531	            # of the core would race with the step that is still inside
 5532	            # the operating system, so it is not started here.
 5533	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
 5534	                                       'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
 5535	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
 5536	            return
 5537	        try:
 5538	            self.core.stop(self.core.stop_reason or 'LOOP_EXIT', force_unknown=True)
 5539	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5540	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
 5541	                                        'error_type': type(error).__name__})
 5542	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
 5543	
 5544	    def _loop(self):
 5545	        self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
 5546	        while True:
 5547	            now = self._clock()
 5548	            if now >= self._deadline_mono:
 5549	                return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
 5550	            if self.core.overflowed():
 5551	                return self._stop('BOUND_EXCEEDED', 4)
 5552	            timeout = min(POLL_SECONDS, max(0.01, self._deadline_mono - now))
 5553	            try:
 5554	                raw = self.transport.read(timeout)
 5555	            except BrokerError:
 5556	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_ERROR', 4)
 5557	            if raw is None:
 5558	                # A missing heartbeat is a secondary signal.  The absolute
 5559	                # deadline stays the authority of the round.
 5560	                if self._wall() - self._last_frame_wall > HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:
 5561	                    return self._stop('HEARTBEAT_MISSING', 4)
 5562	                continue
 5563	            if raw == b'':
 5564	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_EOF', 4)
 5565	            # The hard cap of the channel is checked before the frame is
 5566	            # decoded: a frame that does not fit at all is refused
 5567	            # without being accepted.
 5568	            if self._frames + self._sent_frames + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
 5569	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
 5570	            try:
 5571	                frame = decode_command(raw, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
 5572	                                       candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
 5573	                                       authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
 5574	                                       nonce=self.nonce, last_sequence=self._last_sequence)
 5575	            except CustodyError as error:
 5576	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'channel', 'code': error.code})
 5577	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_REFUSED', 4)
 5578	            # The reservation of the terminal exchange is **consumed** by
 5579	            # the terminal frame itself.  A STOP that arrives near the
 5580	            # limit of the budget is a legal frame: it is never refused
 5581	            # because of the reservation that was kept for it.  A frame
 5582	            # that is not terminal never borrows the reservation and it
 5583	            # fails closed when the total does not fit.
 5584	            terminal = frame['command'] == 'STOP'
 5585	            try:
 5586	                self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
 5587	            except BrokerError:
 5588	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
 5589	            self._frames += 1
 5590	            if self._frames > self._max_frames:
 5591	                return self._stop('FRAME_COUNT_EXCEEDED', 4)
 5592	            # Only a frame that was decoded and that carries the identity
 5593	            # of the round refreshes the heartbeat of the round.  A frame
 5594	            # that was refused never does.
 5595	            self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
 5596	            self._last_alive_wall = self._last_frame_wall
 5597	            self._last_sequence = frame['sequence']
 5598	            if frame['command'] == 'STATUS':
 5599	                self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
 5600	                continue
 5601	            return self._stop('STOP_COMMAND', 0)
 5602	
 5603	    def _stop(self, reason, exit_code):
 5604	        unknown = reason != 'STOP_COMMAND'
 5605	        self.core.stop(reason, force_unknown=unknown)
 5606	        self._reply('STOPPED', self.core.state, self.core.summary(), terminal=True)
 5607	        return exit_code
 5608	
 5609	    def _write_receipt(self, reason):
 5610	        """Write the terminal receipt of the round into the run root.
 5611	
 5612	        The receipt is the evidence that survives a parent that died.  It
 5613	        is written once, it is bounded and it is never replaced by a
 5614	        successful claim.  A failure of the write never replaces the
 5615	        first failure of the round.
 5616	        """
 5617	        evidence = self.core.evidence()
 5618	        evidence['reason'] = reason
 5619	        evidence['channel'] = {'frames_received': self._frames,
 5620	                               'last_sequence_received': self._last_sequence,
 5621	                               'frame_limit': self._max_frames}
 5622	        evidence['deadline_wall'] = self._deadline_wall
 5623	        evidence['deadline_extended'] = False
 5624	        raw = json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
 5625	                         indent=2).encode('utf-8')
 5626	        if len(raw) > MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES:
 5627	            raw = json.dumps({'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-receipt/1',
 5628	                              'reason': reason, 'truncated': True,
 5629	                              'stage_pass_claim': False}).encode('utf-8')
 5630	        path = Path(self.core.run_root) / 'temp' / 'native' / 'custody-terminal.json'
 5631	        try:
 5632	            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 5633	            path.write_bytes(raw)
 5634	        except OSError:
 5635	            return False
 5636	        return True
 5637	
 5638	
 5639	# -- the fixed entry of the broker -----------------------------------------
 5640	
 5641	def build_broker_command(*, plan_path, plan_sha256, run_root, nonce, python=None):
 5642	    """The fixed command of the broker.
 5643	
 5644	    The command starts exactly this broker with one sealed plan document.
 5645	    There is no dynamic import of a caller supplied module and no arbitrary
 5646	    argument vector: the only arguments are the plan, its digest, the run
 5647	    root of the round and the nonce of the session.
 5648	    """
 5649	    script = Path(__file__).resolve()
 5650	    if not script.is_file():
 5651	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_SCRIPT_MISSING')
 5652	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(plan_sha256)):
 5653	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_INVALID')
 5654	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
 5655	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
 5656	    executable = python or sys.executable
 5657	    return [executable, '-I', '-B', str(script),
 5658	            '--plan', str(plan_path), '--plan-sha256', str(plan_sha256),
 5659	            '--run-root', str(run_root), '--nonce', str(nonce)]
 5660	
 5661	
 5662	class BrokerProcess:
 5663	    """The original handle of the broker process of one round.
 5664	
 5665	    The parent keeps the process handle, the pipes of the private channel
 5666	    and the identity of the executable.  The replies of the broker are
 5667	    read through one bounded background reader, so the parent never blocks
 5668	    on the channel and the deadline of the round is never held up by a
 5669	    silent broker.
 5670	
 5671	    The handle is **deadline aware**: it says so with its own marker and
 5672	    it takes the absolute deadline of every write.  A handle that is not
 5673	    deadline aware is never assumed to be deadline aware by the parent.
 5674	    """
 5675	
 5676	    #: The marker of a handle that understands an absolute deadline.
 5677	    channel_deadline_aware = True
 5678	
 5679	    def __init__(self, process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
 5680	                 clock=None, context=None):
 5681	        self._process = process
 5682	        self._channel = channel
 5683	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
 5684	        # The ONE context of the launch.  The close of the broker reads
 5685	        # the absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of
 5686	        # the round from it and it never opens a budget of its own.
 5687	        self._context = context
 5688	        self.pid = process.pid
 5689	        self.executable = executable
 5690	        self.executable_sha256 = executable_sha256
 5691	        # Whether the reader of the channel really stopped when the channel
 5692	        # was closed.  It is ``None`` until the close happened and it stays
 5693	        # ``False`` when the reader was still alive after the bounded join.
 5694	        self.reader_finished = None
 5695	        # The absolute deadline of the round, handed over by the parent.
 5696	        # The parent may only shorten it.
 5697	        self.write_deadline = None
 5698	        self.close_facts = None
 5699	
 5700	    def bind_channel_deadline(self, deadline):
 5701	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the private channel.
 5702	
 5703	        The deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock
 5704	        and it may only shorten the deadline that is already bound: a
 5705	        later deadline is refused.
 5706	        """
 5707	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 5708	        if value is None:
 5709	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID')
 5710	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
 5711	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
 5712	        self.write_deadline = value
 5713	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
 5714	        if callable(binder):
 5715	            binder(value)
 5716	        return value
 5717	
 5718	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
 5719	        """Write one frame of the parent, bounded by the deadline.
 5720	
 5721	        The deadline is explicit and it is checked: a write that is not
 5722	        bounded by an absolute deadline would let a broker that never
 5723	        reads hold the supervisor of the round forever.
 5724	        """
 5725	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 5726	        if value is None:
 5727	            value = self.write_deadline
 5728	        if value is None:
 5729	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_MISSING')
 5730	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
 5731	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
 5732	        return self._channel.write(raw, value)
 5733	
 5734	    def read(self, timeout):
 5735	        return self._channel.read(timeout)
 5736	
 5737	    def close(self, deadline=None):
 5738	        """Close the original handle of the broker.
 5739	
 5740	        The close happens even when the exchange of the stop failed: the
 5741	        resource of this round is released either way.
 5742	
 5743	        The facts of the close are returned and they are named: a handle
 5744	        that was not closed, a reader that did not stop and a process that
 5745	        did not exit are facts of the round and they are never hidden
 5746	        behind a success.  The release of the original process handle is
 5747	        explicit and it is never left to the garbage collector.
 5748	
 5749	        The waits of the close are bounded by **one** absolute deadline:
 5750	        the deadline that the parent bound before it closed the handle.
 5751	        The remaining time is recomputed before every wait, so that a
 5752	        wait that already consumed part of the budget never gets the
 5753	        whole budget again and no layer of the cleanup adds a budget of
 5754	        its own.
 5755	
 5756	        The close may be **repeated** inside the same deadline: an item
 5757	        that was confirmed is never closed a second time and an item
 5758	        that was not confirmed is coordinated again.  The attempt that
 5759	        ended is not a release: only the facts of the close prove it.
 5760	
 5761	        The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
 5762	        round and not a fact of the last attempt: a repeated close keeps
 5763	        the first error that the reader really had, it publishes the
 5764	        source of that error and it never turns a real error into a
 5765	        ``None``.  The resource facts of the close may converge while
 5766	        the error history of the reader stays: a close that returned is
 5767	        not a release and a retry is not an amnesty.
 5768	
 5769	        The **endpoint is the only owner** of the files of the channel.
 5770	        The broker process therefore never closes the standard input and
 5771	        the standard output of the process by itself: it closes the
 5772	        channel, and the channel closes the files that it owns.
 5773	        """
 5774	        value = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
 5775	        budget = max(0.0, value - self._clock())
 5776	        facts = {'channel_closed': False,
 5777	                 'channel_reader_finished': None,
 5778	                 'channel_reader_error': None,
 5779	                 'channel_reader_error_source': None,
 5780	                 'channel_reader_secondary_errors': [],
 5781	                 'channel_overflow': False,
 5782	                 'channel_overflow_facts': {},
 5783	                 'close_cancellation': None,
 5784	                 'channel_retained_workers': 0,
 5785	                 'write_end_closed': False,
 5786	                 'read_end_closed': False,
 5787	                 'stdin_closed': False,
 5788	                 'stdout_closed': False,
 5789	                 'process_exited': False,
 5790	                 'exit_code': None,
 5791	                 'terminated': False,
 5792	                 'process_handle_released': False,
 5793	                 'process_handle_release_method': None,
 5794	                 'worker_thread_handle_released': False,
 5795	                 'cleanup_budget_seconds': budget,
 5796	                 'cleanup_deadline': value}
 5797	        # The facts of a previous attempt are kept: an item that was
 5798	        # confirmed is never closed a second time and its fact stays
 5799	        # true.
 5800	        previous = self.close_facts if isinstance(self.close_facts, dict) else {}
 5801	        for key, confirmed in previous.items():
 5802	            if key in ('cleanup_budget_seconds', 'cleanup_deadline'):
 5803	                continue
 5804	            if (confirmed is True
 5805	                    or (key == 'exit_code' and confirmed is not None)
 5806	                    or key == 'process_handle_release_method'
 5807	                    or key == 'close_cancellation'):
 5808	                facts[key] = confirmed
 5809	        # The channel and its endpoints use the very same absolute
 5810	        # deadline: they never start a wait of their own.
 5811	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
 5812	        if callable(binder):
 5813	            try:
 5814	                binder(value)
 5815	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5816	                pass
 5817	        if not facts['channel_closed']:
 5818	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
 5819	        elif not self._channel_complete(facts):
 5820	            # A previous attempt already called the close of the
 5821	            # channel, but it did not confirm **every** item of it: the
 5822	            # read end, the worker of an endpoint and the original
 5823	            # handle of its thread may still be open.  The items that
 5824	            # were not confirmed are coordinated again, inside the very
 5825	            # same deadline.  A close that returned is not a release.
 5826	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
 5827	        else:
 5828	            channel_facts = None
 5829	        if channel_facts is not None:
 5830	            facts.update(channel_facts)
 5831	        # The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
 5832	        # round: a repeated close never turns a real error of the reader
 5833	        # into a ``None``.  The first error is the error of the round and
 5834	        # a later error is kept beside it as a secondary one.
 5835	        self._keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous)
 5836	        # The files of the channel belong to the endpoints of the
 5837	        # channel.  The fact of their close is the fact of the channel.
 5838	        facts['stdin_closed'] = facts['write_end_closed']
 5839	        facts['stdout_closed'] = facts['read_end_closed']
 5840	        if not facts['process_exited']:
 5841	            try:
 5842	                self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, value - self._clock()))
 5843	                facts['process_exited'] = True
 5844	                facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
 5845	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5846	                # Only the broker that this parent holds is terminated, and
 5847	                # its exit is confirmed afterwards with a bounded wait.
 5848	                try:
 5849	                    self._process.terminate()
 5850	                    facts['terminated'] = True
 5851	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5852	                    pass
 5853	                try:
 5854	                    self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, value - self._clock()))
 5855	                    facts['process_exited'] = True
 5856	                    facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
 5857	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5858	                    pass
 5859	        if not facts['process_handle_released']:
 5860	            facts.update(self._release_process_handle(facts['process_exited']))
 5861	        self.close_facts = facts
 5862	        self.reader_finished = facts['channel_reader_finished']
 5863	        return facts
 5864	
 5865	    @staticmethod
 5866	    def _keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous):
 5867	        """Keep the history of the error of the reader of the channel.
 5868	
 5869	        The **first** error that the reader of the channel really had is
 5870	        the error of the round: every later close keeps it and it is
 5871	        never turned into a ``None`` by a repeated close.  The resource
 5872	        facts of the close may converge, but a real error of the reader
 5873	        is never lost by the retry: the copy of the facts of a previous
 5874	        attempt keeps the facts that are ``True`` and it does **not**
 5875	        keep a code, so the error is restored here from the history
 5876	        itself.
 5877	
 5878	        A later error is a **secondary** error: it is kept beside the
 5879	        first one and it never replaces it.  The source of the error is
 5880	        published with it: ``FIRST_CLOSE`` when the close that is running
 5881	        now is the one that really observed the error and
 5882	        ``REPEATED_CLOSE`` when this attempt carries the error of an
 5883	        earlier one.  The fact of the error itself is the history and it
 5884	        is never replaced by a boolean.
 5885	
 5886	        The named fact of the cancellation of the close is independent
 5887	        from this history and it is never merged with it.
 5888	        """
 5889	        previous = previous if isinstance(previous, dict) else {}
 5890	        first = previous.get('channel_reader_error')
 5891	        current = facts.get('channel_reader_error')
 5892	        secondary = list(previous.get('channel_reader_secondary_errors') or [])
 5893	        if first:
 5894	            if current and current != first:
 5895	                # A second error of the reader is kept beside the first
 5896	                # one: the first error stays the error of the round.
 5897	                if current not in secondary:
 5898	                    secondary.append(current)
 5899	            facts['channel_reader_secondary_errors'] = secondary
 5900	            facts['channel_reader_error'] = first
 5901	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
 5902	            return facts
 5903	        if current:
 5904	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'FIRST_CLOSE'
 5905	        return facts
 5906	
 5907	    @staticmethod
 5908	    def _channel_complete(facts):
 5909	        """Whether the close of the channel confirmed every item of it.
 5910	
 5911	        The items are the two ends of the channel, the reader of the
 5912	        channel, the workers of the endpoints and the original handles
 5913	        of their threads.  A fact that is missing is a missing fact and
 5914	        not a success.
 5915	        """
 5916	        if facts.get('channel_reader_finished') is not True:
 5917	            return False
 5918	        if facts.get('read_end_closed') is not True:
 5919	            return False
 5920	        if facts.get('write_end_closed') is not True:
 5921	            return False
 5922	        if int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) != 0:
 5923	            return False
 5924	        if facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released') is not True:
 5925	            return False
 5926	        return True
 5927	
 5928	    def _close_channel(self, facts):
 5929	        """Close the channel of the round and report its facts.
 5930	
 5931	        The method returns the facts of the close of the channel and it
 5932	        never raises: the failure of the close of the channel is a fact
 5933	        of the round and it is named in the facts.
 5934	        """
 5935	        try:
 5936	            channel_facts = self._channel.close()
 5937	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 5938	            facts['channel_closed'] = False
 5939	            return None
 5940	        facts['channel_closed'] = True
 5941	        facts['channel_reader_finished'] = getattr(self._channel,
 5942	                                                    'reader_finished', None)
 5943	        if not isinstance(channel_facts, dict):
 5944	            return None
 5945	        facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('write_end_closed'))
 5946	        facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('read_end_closed'))
 5947	        facts['channel_reader_error'] = channel_facts.get('reader_error')
 5948	        # The overflow of the channel is a **named** failure of the
 5949	        # round and it travels with the facts of the close: a channel
 5950	        # that overflowed is never a channel that ended normally.
 5951	        facts['channel_overflow'] = bool(channel_facts.get('overflow'))
 5952	        facts['channel_overflow_facts'] = dict(
 5953	            channel_facts.get('overflow_facts') or {})
 5954	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = int(
 5955	            (channel_facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
 5956	            + (channel_facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
 5957	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
 5958	            channel_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
 5959	        return channel_facts
 5960	
 5961	    def _cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
 5962	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of the broker.
 5963	
 5964	        The deadline is the one that the parent bound before it closed
 5965	        the handle.  It may only be shortened, never extended, and every
 5966	        wait of the cleanup recomputes the time that is left.
 5967	
 5968	        The **context** of the launch is read as well: a cleanup that
 5969	        runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
 5970	        deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
 5971	        """
 5972	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 5973	        if value is None:
 5974	            value = self.write_deadline
 5975	        context = self._context
 5976	        if context is not None:
 5977	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
 5978	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
 5979	                if candidate is None:
 5980	                    continue
 5981	                if value is None or candidate < value:
 5982	                    value = candidate
 5983	        if value is None:
 5984	            value = self._clock() + CLEANUP_BUDGET_SECONDS
 5985	        elif self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
 5986	            value = self.write_deadline
 5987	        return value
 5988	
 5989	    def _release_process_handle(self, exited=False):
 5990	        """Release the original process handle, explicitly and by platform.
 5991	
 5992	        The release is recorded and it is never left to the garbage
 5993	        collector: on Windows the original handle of the process is closed
 5994	        through its own handle object and on POSIX the child process has
 5995	        no separate handle that the parent owns.
 5996	
 5997	        The original handle of the process is closed **only** when the
 5998	        exit of the process was really confirmed.  A process whose exit
 5999	        was not confirmed may still be alive and its original handle is
 6000	        the only handle that the parent owns for it: the handle stays
 6001	        with its owner and a later close may release it.  A close that
 6002	        succeeds is never a proof that the process ended.
 6003	        """
 6004	        if not exited:
 6005	            return {'process_handle_released': False,
 6006	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED'}
 6007	        if os.name == 'nt':
 6008	            handle = getattr(self._process, '_handle', None)
 6009	            close = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
 6010	            if close is None:
 6011	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
 6012	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_NO_EXPLICIT_HANDLE'}
 6013	            try:
 6014	                close()
 6015	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6016	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
 6017	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED'}
 6018	            return {'process_handle_released': True,
 6019	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE'}
 6020	        return {'process_handle_released': True,
 6021	                'process_handle_release_method': 'POSIX_PROCESS_EXIT'}
 6022	
 6023	
 6024	class _LaunchAggregate:
 6025	    """The thin persistent owner of **one** launch.
 6026	
 6027	    Every launch of this package has exactly one aggregate and it is
 6028	    created **before** the first side effect of the launch.  It holds
 6029	    the original references of the launch: the process, the channel,
 6030	    the partial endpoints, the workers of the endpoints and the context
 6031	    of the launch.
 6032	
 6033	    The aggregate is the entry that the outer owner of a failed launch
 6034	    uses.  A construction that did not return, a channel whose reader
 6035	    thread did not start and a channel that could not be handed over
 6036	    are found **here** and never through a local variable that is
 6037	    ``None`` at that moment.
 6038	
 6039	    The release of the aggregate is ordered by the **dependency**: the
 6040	    channel owns the files of the process and the worker of an endpoint
 6041	    owns the handle of its thread, so the channel is coordinated first
 6042	    and the process is coordinated after it.  A process whose channel
 6043	    took the files is never closed by the process helper: a file that
 6044	    is closed under a live worker is a file that a worker reads from.
 6045	    """
 6046	
 6047	    def __init__(self, *, launch_id=None, custody=None, clock=None,
 6048	                 context=None):
 6049	        self.launch_id = launch_id or 'launch'
 6050	        self.custody = custody
 6051	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
 6052	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
 6053	        self.process = None
 6054	        self.channel = None
 6055	        self.endpoints = []
 6056	        self.workers = []
 6057	        self.context = context
 6058	        self.facts = {}
 6059	
 6060	    # -- the bindings of the launch ---------------------------------------
 6061	    def bind_process(self, process):
