 6062	        """Bind the original process of this launch."""
 6063	        with self._lock:
 6064	            self.process = process
 6065	        return process
 6066	
 6067	    def bind_channel(self, channel):
 6068	        """Bind the channel of this launch."""
 6069	        with self._lock:
 6070	            if channel is not None:
 6071	                self.channel = channel
 6072	        return channel
 6073	
 6074	    def bind_endpoint(self, endpoint):
 6075	        """Bind one endpoint of this launch."""
 6076	        with self._lock:
 6077	            self.endpoints.append(endpoint)
 6078	        return endpoint
 6079	
 6080	    def bind_worker(self, worker):
 6081	        """Bind one worker of this launch."""
 6082	        with self._lock:
 6083	            self.workers.append(worker)
 6084	        return worker
 6085	
 6086	    def bind_context(self, context):
 6087	        """Bind the ONE context of this launch."""
 6088	        with self._lock:
 6089	            if context is not None:
 6090	                self.context = context
 6091	        return self.context
 6092	
 6093	    # -- the custody protocol of the launcher -----------------------------
 6094	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None):
 6095	        """Take one original resource of this launch.
 6096	
 6097	        The protocol is the protocol of the table of the launcher: the
 6098	        channel of a launch registers itself **before** it builds
 6099	        anything, so that a partial channel is always reachable.
 6100	        """
 6101	        with self._lock:
 6102	            if kind == 'channel' and resource is not None:
 6103	                self.channel = resource
 6104	            elif kind == 'endpoint':
 6105	                self.endpoints.append(resource)
 6106	            elif kind == 'worker':
 6107	                self.workers.append(resource)
 6108	        return resource
 6109	
 6110	    def forget(self, resource):
 6111	        """Leave the launch: the resource was handed over or released."""
 6112	        with self._lock:
 6113	            if self.channel is resource:
 6114	                self.channel = None
 6115	            self.endpoints = [item for item in self.endpoints
 6116	                              if item is not resource]
 6117	            self.workers = [item for item in self.workers
 6118	                            if item is not resource]
 6119	        return None
 6120	
 6121	    def items(self):
 6122	        """The original resources that this launch still owns."""
 6123	        with self._lock:
 6124	            return {'process': self.process, 'channel': self.channel,
 6125	                    'endpoints': list(self.endpoints),
 6126	                    'workers': list(self.workers)}
 6127	
 6128	    # -- the coordinated release ------------------------------------------
 6129	    def release(self, deadline=None):
 6130	        """Coordinate the resources of this launch, in the dependency order.
 6131	
 6132	        The channel is coordinated **first** and the process is
 6133	        coordinated after it.  A close that returned is not a release:
 6134	        the channel is released only when **every** fact of it is
 6135	        closed - the reader, the two ends, the workers and the original
 6136	        handles of their threads.  An incomplete close keeps the
 6137	        channel with its owner and it is named.
 6138	
 6139	        The original handle of the process is released only after the
 6140	        exit of the process was really confirmed.
 6141	
 6142	        The release never raises before every safe step was executed:
 6143	        the original cancellation of the launch is raised at the end and
 6144	        the ordinary failures are named beside it.
 6145	        """
 6146	        released = []
 6147	        retained = []
 6148	        failures = {}
 6149	        cancellations = []
 6150	        channel = self.channel
 6151	        if channel is not None:
 6152	            facts = {}
 6153	            try:
 6154	                facts = channel.close()
 6155	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 6156	                cancellations.append(error)
 6157	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6158	                failures['channel'] = type(error).__name__
 6159	            if not isinstance(facts, dict):
 6160	                facts = {}
 6161	            complete = (bool(facts.get('reader_finished'))
 6162	                        and bool(facts.get('read_end_closed'))
 6163	                        and bool(facts.get('write_end_closed'))
 6164	                        and int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) == 0
 6165	                        and bool(facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released')))
 6166	            if complete:
 6167	                released.append('channel')
 6168	                self.forget(channel)
 6169	                if self.custody is not None:
 6170	                    self.custody.forget(channel)
 6171	            else:
 6172	                # A close that returned is not a release: the channel
 6173	                # keeps its owner and its original reference.
 6174	                failures['channel'] = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE'
 6175	                retained.append('channel')
 6176	                if self.custody is not None:
 6177	                    self.custody.retain('channel', channel,
 6178	                                         'release_incomplete', launch=self)
 6179	        process = self.process
 6180	        if process is not None:
 6181	            # The files of the process belong to the channel when the
 6182	            # channel took them: the process helper never closes them.
 6183	            close_pipes = channel is None
 6184	            outcome = {'failures': {}, 'exited': False}
 6185	            try:
 6186	                outcome = _release_launch_process(
 6187	                    process, deadline=deadline, close_pipes=close_pipes)
 6188	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 6189	                cancellations.append(error)
 6190	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6191	                outcome['failures']['process'] = type(error).__name__
 6192	            failures.update(outcome['failures'])
 6193	            if outcome['exited']:
 6194	                released.append('process')
 6195	                with self._lock:
 6196	                    self.process = None
 6197	                if self.custody is not None:
 6198	                    self.custody.forget(process)
 6199	            else:
 6200	                retained.append('process')
 6201	                if self.custody is not None:
 6202	                    self.custody.retain('process', process, 'release_failed',
 6203	                                         launch=self)
 6204	        self.facts = {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained),
 6205	                      'failures': dict(failures)}
 6206	        if cancellations:
 6207	            # The original cancellation is raised after every safe step
 6208	            # of the release was executed and the ordinary failures stay
 6209	            # named next to it.
 6210	            raise cancellations[0]
 6211	        return self.facts
 6212	
 6213	
 6214	class _LauncherCustody:
 6215	    """The persistent owner of the original resources of a failed launch.
 6216	
 6217	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
 6218	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
 6219	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
 6220	    nobody owns.
 6221	
 6222	    Every original resource of such a launch enters this table **with
 6223	    its original reference** and it stays there until a confirmed
 6224	    release: the round never loses the ownership of a resource that it
 6225	    created, and a pid is never used to find a process again.
 6226	
 6227	    The table is the recovery entry point of the launcher.  It is
 6228	    explicit and it is finite: a caller that wants the resources of a
 6229	    failed launch released asks for it.
 6230	
 6231	    The resources of a launch are coordinated by the **aggregate** of
 6232	    that launch and not by the order of this table: the channel of a
 6233	    launch is closed before the process of that launch, because the
 6234	    channel owns the files of the process.  A resource that belongs to
 6235	    a launch is never released a second time by this table.
 6236	    """
 6237	
 6238	    def __init__(self):
 6239	        self._items = []
 6240	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
 6241	        # The ONE context of the round, when the table is bound to it.
 6242	        # A recovery that runs without a deadline of its own reads the
 6243	        # absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of the
 6244	        # round from it and it never opens a fresh budget.
 6245	        self._context = None
 6246	
 6247	    def bind_context(self, context):
 6248	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to this table."""
 6249	        if context is not None:
 6250	            self._context = context
 6251	        return self._context
 6252	
 6253	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
 6254	        """The ONE deadline that a release of this table really uses."""
 6255	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 6256	        context = self._context
 6257	        if context is not None:
 6258	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
 6259	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
 6260	                if candidate is None:
 6261	                    continue
 6262	                if value is None or candidate < value:
 6263	                    value = candidate
 6264	        return value
 6265	
 6266	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None, launch=None):
 6267	        """Take one original resource into the table of the launcher.
 6268	
 6269	        The table never names the same resource twice: a resource that
 6270	        is already with its owner is not entered a second time and the
 6271	        table converges.
 6272	
 6273	        The ``launch`` is the aggregate that owns the resource.  A
 6274	        resource that belongs to a launch is coordinated by that
 6275	        launch and it is never released a second time by this table.
 6276	        """
 6277	        with self._lock:
 6278	            for item in self._items:
 6279	                if item['kind'] == kind and item['resource'] is resource:
 6280	                    return resource
 6281	            self._items.append({'kind': kind, 'resource': resource,
 6282	                                'detail': detail, 'launch': launch})
 6283	        return resource
 6284	
 6285	    def forget(self, resource):
 6286	        """Leave the table: the resource was handed over to its owner."""
 6287	        with self._lock:
 6288	            remaining = [item for item in self._items
 6289	                         if item['resource'] is not resource]
 6290	            self._items = remaining
 6291	
 6292	    def items(self):
 6293	        """The original resources that the launcher still owns."""
 6294	        with self._lock:
 6295	            return list(self._items)
 6296	
 6297	    def release(self, deadline=None, context=None):
 6298	        """Try to release every original resource of the table.
 6299	
 6300	        The release of every resource is independent of the others: a
 6301	        resource that could not be released stays in the table with its
 6302	        original reference and it is named in the report.
 6303	
 6304	        The **launches** are coordinated first: every aggregate closes
 6305	        the channel of its launch before the process of that launch.
 6306	        The resources that belong to no launch are released afterwards
 6307	        and a resource of a launch is never released twice.
 6308	
 6309	        The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline.
 6310	        A recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a
 6311	        fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
 6312	        """
 6313	        released = []
 6314	        retained = []
 6315	        cancellations = []
 6316	        deadline = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
 6317	        with self._lock:
 6318	            items = list(self._items)
 6319	        keep = []
 6320	        for item in items:
 6321	            if item['kind'] == 'aggregate':
 6322	                launch = item['resource']
 6323	                try:
 6324	                    report = launch.release(deadline=deadline)
 6325	                except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
 6326	                    cancellations.append(error)
 6327	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
 6328	                except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6329	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
 6330	                released.extend(report.get('released') or [])
 6331	                names = list(report.get('retained') or [])
 6332	                retained.extend(names)
 6333	                if names:
 6334	                    keep.append(item)
 6335	                continue
 6336	            if item.get('launch') is not None:
 6337	                # The launch of this resource coordinates it: the table
 6338	                # never releases it a second time.
 6339	                keep.append(item)
 6340	                retained.append(item['kind'])
 6341	                continue
 6342	            resource = item['resource']
 6343	            try:
 6344	                if item['kind'] == 'process':
 6345	                    failures = _terminate_and_close_process(resource,
 6346	                                                            deadline=deadline)
 6347	                    if failures:
 6348	                        keep.append(item)
 6349	                        retained.append(item['kind'])
 6350	                        continue
 6351	                else:
 6352	                    close = getattr(resource, 'close', None)
 6353	                    if callable(close):
 6354	                        close()
 6355	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6356	                keep.append(item)
 6357	                retained.append(item['kind'])
 6358	                continue
 6359	            released.append(item['kind'])
 6360	        with self._lock:
 6361	            self._items = keep
 6362	        if cancellations:
 6363	            raise cancellations[0]
 6364	        return {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained)}
 6365	
 6366	
 6367	#: The one table of the original resources of the launches of this
 6368	#: package.  It is empty after a launch that was handed over and it
 6369	#: names the resources of a launch that was not.
 6370	_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY = _LauncherCustody()
 6371	
 6372	
 6373	def release_retained_launches(deadline=None, *, context=None):
 6374	    """The recovery entry point of the launcher of this package.
 6375	
 6376	    The caller asks for the original resources of the launches that were
 6377	    not handed over to be released, and the report names the resources
 6378	    that could not be released.
 6379	
 6380	    The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline of the
 6381	    round: a recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never
 6382	    waits a fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be
 6383	    alive.
 6384	
 6385	    The deadline of the context is **read again** here: a recovery
 6386	    that runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
 6387	    deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
 6388	    """
 6389	    value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 6390	    if context is not None:
 6391	        _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
 6392	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.release(deadline=value)
 6393	
 6394	
 6395	def _launcher_custody_items():
 6396	    """The original resources that the launcher of this package owns.
 6397	
 6398	    The list is a copy and every entry carries the **original** object:
 6399	    a pid is never used to find a process of the round again.
 6400	    """
 6401	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.items()
 6402	
 6403	
 6404	def _release_launch_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True):
 6405	    """Terminate one process of a launch and report the two facts.
 6406	
 6407	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
 6408	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed when
 6409	    nobody else owns them.
 6410	
 6411	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: a
 6412	    recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed
 6413	    number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
 6414	
 6415	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
 6416	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
 6417	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
 6418	    it and it is never closed under it.
 6419	
 6420	    The report names every step that did not succeed and it names the
 6421	    **exit** of the process as a separate fact: a close that succeeded
 6422	    is never a proof that the process ended.
 6423	    """
 6424	    failures = {}
 6425	    exited = False
 6426	    try:
 6427	        process.terminate()
 6428	    except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6429	        failures['terminate'] = type(error).__name__
 6430	    budget = CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
 6431	    if deadline is not None:
 6432	        try:
 6433	            value = _require_deadline(deadline)
 6434	        except BrokerError:
 6435	            value = None
 6436	        if value is not None:
 6437	            budget = max(0.0, value - time.monotonic())
 6438	    try:
 6439	        process.wait(timeout=budget)
 6440	        exited = True
 6441	    except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6442	        failures['wait'] = type(error).__name__
 6443	    if exited and os.name == 'nt':
 6444	        # The original handle of the process is closed explicitly and it
 6445	        # is never left to the garbage collector.  A handle that could
 6446	        # not be closed keeps its owner.
 6447	        handle = getattr(process, '_handle', None)
 6448	        closer = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
 6449	        if callable(closer):
 6450	            try:
 6451	                closer()
 6452	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6453	                failures['handle'] = type(error).__name__
 6454	    if close_pipes:
 6455	        for name in ('stdin', 'stdout', 'stderr'):
 6456	            stream = getattr(process, name, None)
 6457	            if stream is None:
 6458	                continue
 6459	            try:
 6460	                stream.close()
 6461	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6462	                failures[name] = type(error).__name__
 6463	    return {'failures': failures, 'exited': exited}
 6464	
 6465	
 6466	def _terminate_and_close_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True):
 6467	    """Terminate one process of this round and close its pipes.
 6468	
 6469	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
 6470	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed.  The
 6471	    method never raises: it is the last step of a failure and it never
 6472	    replaces the failure that is on the way out.
 6473	
 6474	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: a
 6475	    recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed
 6476	    number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
 6477	
 6478	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
 6479	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
 6480	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
 6481	    it and it is never closed under it.
 6482	
 6483	    The report names every step that did not succeed, so that the
 6484	    caller can keep the original reference of a resource that is still
 6485	    open.
 6486	    """
 6487	    return _release_launch_process(process, deadline=deadline,
 6488	                                   close_pipes=close_pipes)['failures']
 6489	
 6490	
 6491	def _adopt_broker_process(process, *, channel_factory, executable,
 6492	                          executable_sha256, deadline=None, aggregate=None,
 6493	                          custody=None):
 6494	    """Take the original process of the broker into the custody of a channel.
 6495	
 6496	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
 6497	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
 6498	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
 6499	    nobody owns.
 6500	
 6501	    The **channel** is the only owner of the files of the process.  A
 6502	    channel that exists and that could not be handed over is closed by
 6503	    the launcher through the channel and never by closing the standard
 6504	    input and the standard output of the process by itself: a file that
 6505	    is closed under a live worker of the channel is a file that a
 6506	    worker is reading from.
 6507	
 6508	    The **aggregate** of the launch is the owner that the outer code
 6509	    uses: a partial channel - a channel whose reader thread did not
 6510	    start - is found through the aggregate and never through a local
 6511	    variable that is ``None`` at that moment.
 6512	
 6513	    A resource that could not be released keeps its **original**
 6514	    reference in the table of the launcher: the session never received
 6515	    the process and the round never loses the ownership of it.
 6516	
 6517	    The handover happens exactly once: the process that the channel
 6518	    received is the process that the parent owns.
 6519	    """
 6520	    table = custody if custody is not None else _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY
 6521	    launch = aggregate if aggregate is not None else _LaunchAggregate(
 6522	        custody=table)
 6523	    launch.bind_process(process)
 6524	    table.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
 6525	    channel = None
 6526	    try:
 6527	        channel = channel_factory()
 6528	        if channel is not None:
 6529	            launch.bind_channel(channel)
 6530	        broker = BrokerProcess(process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
 6531	                               context=launch.context)
 6532	    except BaseException:
 6533	        # The resources of the launch are coordinated by the aggregate:
 6534	        # the channel first and the process after it.  The resources
 6535	        # that could not be released keep their original reference and
 6536	        # their owner.
 6537	        try:
 6538	            launch.release(deadline=deadline)
 6539	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
 6540	            # The cancellation of the release never replaces the
 6541	            # original failure of the launch: the original object
 6542	            # continues to propagate.
 6543	            pass
 6544	        except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6545	            pass
 6546	        raise
 6547	    # The handover happened: the launcher does not own the process of
 6548	    # the round any more.
 6549	    table.forget(process)
 6550	    table.forget(channel)
 6551	    table.forget(launch)
 6552	    launch.forget(channel)
 6553	    launch.forget(process)
 6554	    return broker
 6555	
 6556	
 6557	def launch_broker(plan, run_root, nonce, *, context=None):
 6558	    """Start the broker process of one round and keep its original handle.
 6559	
 6560	    The launcher is a Windows only operation.  It refuses on every other
 6561	    platform before anything is started: a broker that cannot be started
 6562	    on Windows is never replaced by an unconstrained process and never by
 6563	    a simulation that pretends to be one.
 6564	
 6565	    The nonce and the plan are checked **before** the first directory and
 6566	    the first file of the round exist, so that a refused launch leaves
 6567	    nothing behind.
 6568	
 6569	    The ONE context of the round is held **before** the first side
 6570	    effect of the launch: the creation of the process, the reading of
 6571	    the executable, the channel and its endpoints all read the deadline
 6572	    of the round from that context.  A launcher that waits for the end
 6573	    of the launcher to bind the deadline would leave a window in which
 6574	    the process of the round runs without a budget.
 6575	
 6576	    The **aggregate** of the launch is created before the process and it
 6577	    holds every original resource of the launch: a failure at any point
 6578	    of the launch finds the partial owner of the launch through it.
 6579	    """
 6580	    if os.name != 'nt':
 6581	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLATFORM_UNSUPPORTED')
 6582	    if plan is None:
 6583	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID')
 6584	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
 6585	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
 6586	    # The aggregate of this launch and the deadline of the round are
 6587	    # taken **before** the first side effect of the launch.
 6588	    launch = _LaunchAggregate(custody=_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY, context=context)
 6589	    launch.bind_context(context)
 6590	    # The table of the launcher reads the deadline of the round from
 6591	    # the same context: a recovery of the launcher never opens a fresh
 6592	    # budget of its own.
 6593	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
 6594	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
 6595	    deadline = None
 6596	    if context is not None:
 6597	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
 6598	    root = Path(run_root)
 6599	    raw = plan.sealed_bytes()
 6600	    digest = plan.sealed_sha256()
 6601	    directory = root / 'temp' / 'native'
 6602	    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 6603	    plan_path = directory / 'custody-plan.json'
 6604	    with plan_path.open('xb') as stream:
 6605	        stream.write(raw)
 6606	    command = build_broker_command(plan_path=plan_path, plan_sha256=digest,
 6607	                                   run_root=root, nonce=nonce)
 6608	    process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
 6609	                               stderr=None, close_fds=True)
 6610	    # The original process of the round is with the launcher from the
 6611	    # moment of its creation.  The window between the creation of the
 6612	    # process and the handover of the channel is not a window without an
 6613	    # owner: a cancellation of the reading of the executable leaves the
 6614	    # process with its owner and it never disappears.
 6615	    launch.bind_process(process)
 6616	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
 6617	    executable = Path(command[0])
 6618	    try:
 6619	        executable_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
 6620	    except OSError:
 6621	        executable_sha256 = None
 6622	    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
 6623	        # The cancellation of the reading of the executable is cleaned
 6624	        # up and it continues to propagate.  The process keeps its owner
 6625	        # in the table of the launcher and the recovery of the launcher
 6626	        # is the entry that releases it.
 6627	        raise
 6628	
 6629	    def channel_factory():
 6630	        """Build the channel of this launch with the context of the round.
 6631	
 6632	        The channel registers itself with the **aggregate** of the
 6633	        launch before it builds anything: a channel whose reader
 6634	        thread did not start is found by the outer owner through the
 6635	        aggregate and the files of the process are never closed by the
 6636	        process helper.
 6637	        """
 6638	        channel = StreamChannel(process.stdout, process.stdin,
 6639	                                custody=launch, context=context)
 6640	        if deadline is not None:
 6641	            channel.bind_deadline(deadline)
 6642	        return channel
 6643	
 6644	    return _adopt_broker_process(
 6645	        process,
 6646	        channel_factory=channel_factory,
 6647	        executable=str(executable), executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
 6648	        deadline=deadline, aggregate=launch)
 6649	
 6650	
 6651	def read_sealed_plan(path, digest):
 6652	    """Read one sealed plan document and check its digest."""
 6653	    target = Path(path)
 6654	    if not target.is_file():
 6655	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_MISSING')
 6656	    with target.open('rb') as stream:
 6657	        raw = stream.read(MAX_PLAN_BYTES + 1)
 6658	    if len(raw) > MAX_PLAN_BYTES:
 6659	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_TOO_LARGE')
 6660	    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest:
 6661	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_DRIFT')
 6662	    try:
 6663	        document = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'))
 6664	    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as error:
 6665	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID') from error
 6666	    return document
 6667	
 6668	
 6669	def main(argv=None):
 6670	    """The production entry of the broker.
 6671	
 6672	    The entry is fixed: one sealed plan, its digest, the run root of the
 6673	    round and the nonce of the session.  The plan is validated again here,
 6674	    because the broker never trusts a document that it did not check.
 6675	    """
 6676	    import argparse
 6677	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 6678	    parser.add_argument('--plan', required=True)
 6679	    parser.add_argument('--plan-sha256', required=True)
 6680	    parser.add_argument('--run-root', required=True)
 6681	    parser.add_argument('--nonce', required=True)
 6682	    arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
 6683	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(arguments.nonce)):
 6684	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_NONCE_INVALID\n')
 6685	        sys.stderr.flush()
 6686	        return 2
 6687	    document = read_sealed_plan(arguments.plan, arguments.plan_sha256)
 6688	    try:
 6689	        plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
 6690	    except CustodyError as error:
 6691	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
 6692	        sys.stderr.flush()
 6693	        return 2
 6694	    if plan.sealed_sha256() != arguments.plan_sha256:
 6695	        # The seal of the plan that the broker validated is not the seal
 6696	        # that the parent wrote: the document drifted on its way here.
 6697	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_PLAN_SEAL_DRIFT\n')
 6698	        sys.stderr.flush()
 6699	        return 2
 6700	    try:
 6701	        win32 = WindowsApi()
 6702	    except BrokerError as error:
 6703	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
 6704	        sys.stderr.flush()
 6705	        return 2
 6706	    core = BrokerCore(win32=win32, plan=plan, run_root=arguments.run_root)
 6707	    # The ONE context of the round is handed to the transport of the
 6708	    # channel and to its endpoints: the channel, the endpoints and the
 6709	    # workers of the endpoints read the same absolute deadline and none
 6710	    # of them keeps a budget of its own.
 6711	    transport = StreamChannel(sys.stdin.buffer, sys.stdout.buffer, api=win32,
 6712	                              context=core.context)
 6713	    loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=transport, plan=plan,
 6714	                      nonce=arguments.nonce)
 6715	    try:
 6716	        return loop.run()
 6717	    finally:
 6718	        # The round releases its own resources in the loop; the entry only
 6719	        # closes the channel and never swallows a failure of the round.
 6720	        try:
 6721	            transport.close()
 6722	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
 6723	            pass
 6724	
 6725	
 6726	if __name__ == '__main__':
 6727	    sys.exit(main())
